We introduce GradAudit, a gradient-based auditing framework that examines internal optimization dynamics rather than treating VLLMs as black boxes. Our approach builds on a key observation: model parameters converge to regions where gradients on training samples become stable and well-aligned, whereas gradients on non-training samples remain noisy and inconsistent. By analyzing these gradient signatures, GradAudit achieves strong separability and detects genuine image-text associations learned during training, not merely individual modality membership. Empirically, across both medical and general-domain datasets, GradAudit substantially outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both pretraining and fine-tuning VLLMs. In a case study employing copyrighted content, we show that existing training data detection methods not only underestimate the extent of unauthorized data usage, but that this underestimation becomes more pronounced as models become more recent and more advanced.

</details>


### [102] [AerialFusionMapNet: Online HD Map Construction with Aerial-Onboard BEV Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24784)
*Daniel Lengerer,Mathias Pechinger,Klaus Bogenberger,Carsten Markgraf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 AerialFusionMapNet，一种融合航拍与车载传感器的在线高清地图构建框架，核心创新在于采用结构化两阶段训练策略，显式增强航拍特征贡献。在 nuScenes 地理划分上达到 54.7 mAP，较先前基线提升 5.9 个绝对百分点（+12.1% 相对提升），表明训练策略设计比架构复杂度对融合效果影响更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作虽已证明航拍-车载融合可提升在线 HD 地图构建性能，但传统端到端融合方式未能充分挖掘航拍表示中的结构化信息。作者希望探索如何更有效地将航拍影像的结构化先验融入统一融合框架。

Method: 提出 AerialFusionMapNet 框架，核心是结构化两阶段训练策略：第一阶段预训练或引导航拍分支学习结构化特征表示，第二阶段在统一管道中将航拍特征与车载传感器特征进行融合，从而显式增强航拍特征的贡献。该方法侧重于训练策略设计，而非增加架构复杂度。

Result: 在 nuScenes 地理划分数据集上，AerialFusionMapNet 达到最高 54.7 mAP，相较先前航拍-车载融合基线（48.8 mAP）绝对提升 +5.9，相对提升 +12.1%。

Conclusion: 结构化训练设计（而非单纯增加架构复杂度）对充分释放航拍影像在在线 HD 地图构建中的潜力更为关键。该结论为多模态融合任务的研究方向提供了重要启示。

Abstract: High-resolution aerial imagery has recently emerged as a complementary modality for automated driving perception and has shown potential to improve birds-eye-view (BEV) scene understanding when fused with onboard sensors. Prior work demonstrated performance gains for online high-definition (HD) map construction through aerial-onboard fusion; however, conventional end-to-end fusion does not fully exploit the structural information contained in aerial representations. In this work, we introduce AerialFusionMapNet, a fusion-based mapping framework with a structured two-stage training strategy that explicitly enhances the contribution of aerial features within a unified pipeline. The proposed training scheme enables more effective integration of structural aerial priors. On the nuScenes geographic split, AerialFusionMapNet achieves up to 54.7 mAP, improving over prior aerial-onboard fusion baselines from 48.8 mAP by +5.9 absolute and +12.1% relative. The results suggest that structured training design, rather than increased architectural complexity, plays a more decisive role in unlocking the full potential of aerial imagery for online HD map construction. Code and trained models are available at https://github.com/DriverlessMobility/AerialFusionMapNet.

</details>


### [103] [Counting Trees from Satellite Imagery with Noisy Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24786)
*Dimitri Gominski,Maurice Mugabowindekwe,Qiue Xu,Xiaowei Tong,Martin Brandt,Hieu Le,Rasmus Fensholt,Dimitris Samaras,Loic Landrieu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将卫星图像中的树木计数建模为基于非平衡最优传输（Unbalanced Optimal Transport）的空间密度匹配问题，并引入自校正机制利用传输残差逐步优化噪声监督，在覆盖三大洲、2.3万平方公里的TinyTrees基准上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星图像中的单棵树木计数面临两大核心困难：(1) 孤立树木可识别，但密林中树冠边界模糊，导致"单棵树"概念本身难以精确定义；(2) 大规模人工标注成本过高，而从机载LiDAR派生的监督信号噪声大、难以有效利用。现有检测、回归和分布匹配方法均无法同时兼顾孤立树的精确定位与密林的鲁棒密度估计。

Method: (1) 将树木计数形式化为空间密度匹配问题，采用非平衡最优传输（UOT）作为监督信号——UOT天然能够同时处理精确的点对点匹配（孤立树）和松弛的质量分布匹配（密林区域）；(2) 提出自校正机制，利用传输残差（transport residuals）在训练过程中逐步识别和修正噪声标注，实现可靠性感知的监督学习。

Result: 在TinyTrees基准上全面评估：该基准覆盖三大洲、三种卫星传感器（共23,000平方公里），包含超过2.15亿树木标注（其中77.3万为人工验证实例）。所提方法在各项指标上一致超越基于检测、基于回归和基于传输的分布匹配基线方法。

Conclusion: 非平衡最优传输为大规模卫星图像树木计数提供了有效的数学框架，既解决了密林中树冠边界模糊带来的定义困难，又通过自校正机制缓解了噪声监督问题。该方法在TinyTrees基准上的全面验证表明，其具备跨传感器、跨地域的鲁棒泛化能力。

Abstract: Counting individual trees is a fundamental task for environmental monitoring, yet remains largely unexplored with satellite imagery. At these resolutions, isolated trees may still be identifiable, but crown boundaries become ambiguous in dense forests, making the notion of an individual tree inherently ill-defined. Moreover, large-scale manual annotations of individual trees are prohibitively expensive. While scalable supervision can be derived from airborne LiDAR, the resulting annotations are noisy and difficult to exploit effectively.
  We address these challenges by formulating tree counting as a spatial density matching problem supervised through Unbalanced Optimal Transport. This formulation naturally accommodates both precise localization of isolate trees and robust density estimation in dense forests. We further introduce a self-correction mechanism that leverages transport residuals to progressively refine noisy supervision during training.
  We evaluate our approach on TinyTrees, a new benchmark spanning three continents and three satellite sensors, comprising over 215 million tree annotations (including 773K manually verified instances) across 23,000 sq.km. Our method consistently outperforms detection-based, regression-based, and transport-based distribution-matching baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of unbalanced transport and reliability-aware supervision for large-scale tree counting from satellite imagery. Code, data and models are available at https://github.com/dgominski/treematch.

</details>


### [104] [Pocket-SLAM: Rendering-Area-Aware Pruning for Memory-Efficient 3DGS-SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24796)
*Leshu Li,Jie Peng,Yang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于渲染区域感知的剪枝策略，通过移除对有效渲染区域贡献小的高斯点来降低3DGS-SLAM的内存占用，在大规模室外场景中实现超60%内存削减和2倍以上FPS提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS-SLAM在自动驾驶等大规模场景中面临严峻的内存膨胀问题——高斯点随时间持续累积导致内存消耗不断增长，而现有基于不透明度或梯度幅值的高斯级启发式剪枝无法有效针对内存冗余的根源。

Method: 提出渲染区域感知剪枝策略（rendering-area-aware pruning），根据高斯点对有效渲染区域的贡献度进行选择性移除，而非依赖传统的不透明度或梯度等逐高斯启发式指标，直接针对内存冗余源头进行削减。

Result: 在EuRoC和KITTI数据集上，该方法在大规模室外场景中一致优于现有剪枝方法，实现超过60%的内存削减和2倍以上的FPS提升，同时保持定位与建图精度。

Conclusion: 渲染区域感知剪枝是将3DGS-SLAM扩展到真实自动驾驶场景的一个有前景的方向。代码已开源。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has garnered significant attention in Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) due to its advances in capturing fine-grained geometry features and synthesizing novel views. For SLAM in large-scale scenes, such as autonomous driving, 3DGS-SLAM faces a critical limitation: memory consumption increases continuously over time as Gaussian points accumulate, leading to poor memory efficiency and limiting its applicability. In this work, we propose a rendering-area-aware pruning strategy that selectively removes Gaussians based on their contribution to the effective rendering area, rather than solely relying on Gaussian-level heuristics such as opacity or gradient magnitude. This perspective directly targets the sources of memory redundancy, effectively reducing the peak memory footprint of 3DGS-SLAM during runtime. Evaluations on the EuRoC and KITTI datasets demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms existing pruning approaches in large-scale outdoor scenes, achieving over 60% memory reduction and more than 2 times FPS improvement while preserving localization and mapping accuracy. These results highlight rendering-area-aware pruning as a promising direction for scaling 3DGS-SLAM to real-world autonomous driving scenarios. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/UMN-ZhaoLab/Pocket-SLAM.git.

</details>


### [105] [EG-VQA: Benchmarking Verifiable Video Question Answering with Grounded Temporal Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24797)
*Linpeng Huang,Weixing Chen,Zexin Chen,Yang Liu,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出证据锚定的视频问答基准EG-VQA，包含2067个视频和11838个带细粒度时序证据标注的QA对，引入统一评估指标EG-F1，揭示现有模型在答案正确性与证据定位之间的根本性脱节，并构建EG-Reasoner模型通过显式证据监督弥合这一差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VideoQA基准仅评估答案正确性，忽视了预测结果是否基于相关视频证据的锚定。答案生成与证据理解之间的脱节，阻碍了可解释且可靠的视频理解系统的发展。

Method: 构建EG-VQA基准（2067视频/11838 QA对），每个QA对显式标注支持性时序证据；提出EG-F1统一评估指标，联合衡量时序对齐与语义一致性；设计EG-Reasoner模型，通过显式证据监督进行证据锚定推理训练。

Result: 即使强闭源模型也难以准确锚定预测证据，暴露出答案正确性与忠实证据定位之间的根本性差距。EG-Reasoner在开源模型中达到最优性能，在反事实问题等推理密集型任务上表现尤为突出，与闭源系统具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 单纯扩展模型规模不足以实现鲁棒的视频理解，结构化的证据监督对于开发更可靠、可解释的VideoQA系统至关重要。

Abstract: Recent advances in Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs) have yielded promising performance on video question answering (VideoQA). Nevertheless, existing benchmarks are predominantly evaluated through answer correctness, while the grounding of predictions in relevant video evidence remains largely unexamined. This disconnect between answer generation and evidence understanding motivates the construction of the Evidence-Grounded Video Question Answering Benchmark (EG-VQA), an open-ended evaluation protocol in which each QA pair is explicitly annotated with supporting temporal evidence, thereby requiring joint reasoning and precise evidence localization. EG-VQA is comprised of 2,067 videos and 11,838 QA pairs with fine-grained evidence annotations. To evaluate predicted evidence, Evidence-Grounded F1 (EG-F1) is introduced as a unified metric in which temporal alignment and semantic consistency against ground-truth evidence are jointly measured. Experimental evaluation reveals that even strong proprietary models struggle to accurately ground their predictions, exposing a fundamental discrepancy between answer correctness and faithful evidence localization. To bridge this gap, EG-Reasoner, an evidence-grounded reasoning model trained with explicit supervision, is proposed. State-of-the-art performance is achieved among open-source models, with results competitive against proprietary systems, particularly pronounced gains are observed on reasoning-intensive tasks such as counterfactual questions. These findings demonstrate that scaling alone is insufficient for robust video understanding and that structured evidence supervision is essential for the development of more reliable and interpretable VideoQA systems.

</details>


### [106] [OrbitForge: Text-to-3D Scene Generation via Reconstruction-Anchored Video Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24799)
*Chenrui Fan,Paolo Favaro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OrbitForge 提出一种将单段文本生成视频转换为完整闭环轨道3D高斯泼溅场景的方法，无需任务特定微调，通过渐进式重建和缺失视角补全，在覆盖范围和质量上显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到视频模型生成的视频虽质量高，但存在相机运动不可控、视角覆盖不完整、帧间时空不一致等问题，无法直接得到可靠的3D资产。需要一个方法将这些开放世界视频先验转化为3D一致的完整场景。

Method: OrbitForge 包含三个核心步骤：(1) 使用可变形高斯泼溅（Deformable GS）结合 MedianGS 鲁棒代理，从首段生成视频获得初步3D重建；(2) 从预设闭环轨道渲染视角，检测缺失视角；(3) 利用冻结的文本到视频模型仅补全缺失视角，最终重建为完整的3D高斯泼溅场景。整个过程无需任务特定的视频或多视图微调，也无需逐提示的分数蒸馏优化。

Result: 在基于T3Bench的300个冻结提示的评测上，OrbitForge重建达到359.0度的中位视角跨度，将原本不被支持区间的Q10 ImageReward从8.07提升至16.36（相对仅MedianGS重建），同时在覆盖-质量权衡上与VideoMV保持竞争力。

Conclusion: OrbitForge证明了利用冻结视频先验加3D重建锚定可以有效提升生成视频的3D一致性，并强调覆盖感知评估的重要性——仅靠局部平滑性会奖励那些从未尝试完整轨道覆盖的方法。

Abstract: Generic text-to-video models can be used as rich open-world scene priors. Despite the high quality of today's generated videos, they do not directly yield reliable 3D assets: camera motion is difficult to control, view coverage is partial, and frames often contain inconsistencies across time. We introduce OrbitForge, an adapter built from frozen video priors and per-prompt Gaussian Splatting reconstruction optimization that converts a single text-generated video into a canonical closed-orbit 3D Gaussian Splatting scene. We use 3D reconstruction as an anchor to improve the 3D consistency of the generated video. We obtain a preliminary 3D reconstruction from a first generated video via Deformable Gaussian Splatting with a robust MedianGS proxy. We render views from a prescribed orbit to detect missing viewpoints. OrbitForge uses the text-to-video model to complete only the missing views, and reconstructs the completed orbit into a final Gaussian Splatting scene. This design requires no task-specific video or multiview fine-tuning, avoids per-prompt score-distillation optimization, and does not progressively generate views one step at a time. We further argue that this setting demands coverage-aware evaluation: local smoothness alone rewards methods that never attempt a full orbit. On a frozen 300-prompt T3Bench-derived audit, OrbitForge reconstruction attains a 359.0-degree measured median span, raises originally unsupported-bin Q10 ImageReward from 8.07 to 16.36 relative to MedianGS-only reconstruction, while remaining competitive with VideoMV on the coverage-quality.

</details>


### [107] [DDStereo: Efficient Dual Decoder Transformers for Stereo 3D Road Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24805)
*Shiyi Mu,Zichong Gu,Zhiqi Ai,Yilin Gao,Shugong Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DDStereo 是一种基于双解码器立体 Transformer 的实时开放集 3D 目标检测方法，首次在立体视觉领域实现与单目方法相当的实时推理速度，同时在闭集和开放集协议下均达到最优精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前立体 3D 目标检测面临两个关键挑战：一是推理速度远低于单目方法，无法满足实时性需求；二是立体视觉领域的开放集检测研究几乎处于空白状态。本文旨在同时解决实时性能与开放集泛化能力这两个安全关键问题。

Method: 提出 DDStereo（Dual-Decoder Stereo Transformer），核心设计包括：(1) 两个轻量化解码器分支——一个用于开放集前景 2D 检测，另一个用于 3D 属性回归；(2) 两个解码器共享目标级查询（object-level queries），实现统一的目标级对齐；(3) 设计紧凑的视差特征提取器和精简的解码器架构以提升推理效率。

Result: 在公开立体 3D 基准数据集上，DDStereo 在闭集和开放集两种协议下均取得最先进的精度；推理速度超越现有立体 3D 检测器，首次实现与单目方法相当的实时性能。

Conclusion: DDStereo 成功弥合了立体 3D 检测在实时性与开放集泛化方面的双重差距，为立体视觉在安全关键场景中的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Stereo-based 3D object detection still faces two critical safety challenges: real-time performance and open-set generalization. Existing stereo 3D methods typically achieve twice the accuracy of monocular methods but suffer from significantly lower inference speeds, making them unsuitable for real-time applications. Meanwhile, recent advances in open-world detection have introduced open-set and open-vocabulary algorithms in monocular 2D and 3D settings, yet stereo-based open-set detection remains largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose DDStereo, a novel Dual-Decoder Stereo Transformer for real-time open-set 3D object detection. DDStereo features two lightweight decoder branches: one for open-set foreground 2D detection and the other for 3D attribute regression. These decoders share object-level queries to achieve unified target-level alignment. To enhance inference efficiency, we designed a compact disparity feature extractor and a streamlined decoder architecture. Experiments on public stereo 3D benchmarks demonstrate that DDStereo achieves state-of-the-art accuracy under both closed-set and open-set protocols. Notably, our method surpasses existing stereo 3D detectors in inference speed and, for the first time, achieves real-time performance comparable to monocular approaches.

</details>


### [108] [High-Fidelity Synthetic Transmission Electron Microscopy Image Generation Using Diffusion Probabilistic Models for Data-Limited Semiconductor Metrology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24817)
*Johannes Boehm,Bappaditya Dey*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM）的合成TEM图像生成框架，通过渐进式图像块训练策略（仅需15张真实样本即可从头训练），结合TrivialAugment增强、跨工艺域迁移、分类器引导和RePaint式修复等技术，生成符合FAB计量要求的高保真全尺寸TEM图像。此外，还将DDPM特征表示复用于语义分割任务。合成图像达到MS-SSIM > 0.98的质量，可支持缺陷检测、分割和计量等下游机器学习任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进半导体节点对TEM的需求激增，但破坏性制样、缓慢成像和高昂成本严重限制了可用于下游机器学习训练的数据集多样性与规模。现有生成模型往往无法捕捉TEM特有的噪声、结构细节和随机变异性，因此需要一种在极端数据稀缺条件下仍能生成高质量、物理真实的合成TEM图像的方法。

Method: 采用去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM）作为核心生成框架：(1) 渐进式图像块训练策略，从低分辨率图像块逐步扩展到全尺寸图像，仅需15个样本即可从头训练；(2) 集成定制的TrivialAugment数据增强；(3) 跨工艺域迁移技术；(4) 分类器引导机制；(5) RePaint风格的图像修复（inpainting）；(6) 将DDPM编码器的特征图分区，复用于语义分割，获取连贯的区域掩膜。

Result: 合成TEM图像在定量指标上达到MS-SSIM > 0.98，定性专家评估结果与结构相似性指标一致。生成的图像保持了全局结构和空间关系，符合FAB计量要求，并展现出充分的统计与物理真实性，可用于下游的缺陷检测、分割和计量等ML训练任务。

Conclusion: 该DDPM框架在极端数据稀缺（仅15张真实图像）条件下成功实现了高保真合成TEM图像生成，不仅能生成满足工业计量标准的全尺寸图像，其学习到的特征表示还可迁移至分割任务，为半导体制造中的缺陷检测、计量分析等下游ML应用提供了可行的数据增强方案。

Abstract: Advanced semiconductor nodes drastically increased demand for Transmission Electron Microscopy (TEM), yet destructive sample preparation, slow imaging and high costs severely limit the availability of diverse datasets needed for downstream machine learning (ML). Synthetic data generation is becoming essential, but current generative models often miss TEM-specific noise, structural detail, and stochastic variability crucial for evaluation. We present a Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Model (DDPM) framework for synthetic TEM image generation under extreme data scarcity. A progressive patch-based training strategy scales from low-resolution patches to full images, enabling from-scratch training with only 15 samples. We integrate a custom TrivialAugment adaptation, cross-process domain transfer, classifier guidance, and RePaint-style inpainting, culminating in full-image generation that preserves global structural and spatial relationships in compliance with FAB metrology requirements. Beyond synthesis, we repurpose DDPM feature representations for segmentation, partitioning encoder feature maps to obtain coherent region masks. Our synthetic images achieve up to MS-SSIM > 0.98 and qualitative expert assessment consistent with structural similarity results, facilitating downstream ML training for defect detection, segmentation, and metrology while preserving statistical and physical realism.

</details>


### [109] [GeoT2V-Bench: Benchmarking 3D Consistency in Text-to-Video Models via 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24829)
*Chenrui Fan,Paolo Favaro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GeoT2V-Bench，一个基于重建的诊断基准，用于评估相机提示的文本到视频模型是否能支撑显式刚性3D重建，发现不同评估维度之间常存在分歧，揭示了生成视频作为静态3D场景采集时的互补失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相机提示的T2V模型虽在视觉上合理，但生成的帧未必能作为单一静态3D场景的连贯多视图证据。缺乏系统性的基准来诊断生成视频是否真正具备可重建的3D一致性。

Method: 构建GeoT2V-Bench流程：(1)用VGGT风格几何估计方法估计每帧相机内参和位姿；(2)拟合DeformableGS（变形高斯泼溅）；(3)通过时间中值聚合导出静态MedianGS代理；(4)沿估计相机路径渲染该代理。输出连续重建剖面，涵盖表观图像运动、估计轨迹行为、MedianGS静态渲染误差、静态渲染流一致性、以及灵活与静态拟合之间的差距。

Result: 在12个开放权重模型配置和80个GeCo-Eval静态场景提示上，完成3,840次重建的四种子公平格式评估。发现可见运动、静态渲染误差、流一致性和灵活-vs-静态行为之间经常不一致，揭示了互补的失效模式。

Conclusion: GeoT2V-Bench能够捕捉生成视频在作为全局静态场景采集时出现的多种互补失效模式，为T2V模型的3D一致性提供了细粒度的诊断工具。

Abstract: Camera-prompted text-to-video (T2V) models are increasingly used to synthesize virtual camera captures, such as orbiting objects or moving through static scenes. For these outputs, visual plausibility is insufficient: the generated frames should also provide coherent multi-view evidence for a single static 3D scene. We introduce GeoT2V-Bench, a reconstruction-based diagnostic benchmark for evaluating whether camera-prompted T2V clips can support explicit rigid 3D reconstruction. Our pipeline estimates per-frame camera intrinsics and poses with VGGT-style geometry estimation, fits DeformableGS, derives a static MedianGS proxy by temporal-median aggregation, and renders this proxy along the estimated camera path. Instead of producing a pass/fail label or a single scalar score, GeoT2V-Bench reports a continuous reconstruction profile covering apparent image motion, estimated trajectory behavior, MedianGS static rendering error, static-render flow agreement, and the gap between flexible and static fits. On a fair-format four-seed evaluation with 3,840 completed reconstructions from 12 open-weight model configurations and 80 GeCo-Eval static-scene prompts, we find that visible motion, static rendering error, flow agreement, and flexible-vs-static behavior often disagree. GeoT2V-Bench therefore captures complementary failure modes that emerge when generated videos are tested as global static-scene acquisitions.

</details>


### [110] [Bridging the Manifold Gap: Riemannian Residual Line Search for One-Step Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24844)
*Hongzhu Yi,Zhongtian Luo,Tong Li,Yiyan Fan,Jungang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种后处理候选选择方法Riemannian Residual Line Search，通过在能量场输运基础上估计局部时间曲率并构建残差路径，从候选图像中选取CLIP对齐最优者，在PIE-Bench++上达到一步更新算法中的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一步扩散编辑器面临两难：更新强度需足够强以实现目标提示词，又需足够保守以保留源图像，而固定的更新强度无法同时满足所有编辑类型。作者将此视为后处理候选选择问题，而非设计新编辑模型。

Method: 提出Riemannian Residual Line Search方法：(1) 估计提示词-增量场的局部时间曲率，将修正方向投影回原始一阶能量场输运估计的更新范数上，构建更强的编辑；(2) 从源图像到该强编辑构建一条小的残差路径；(3) 保留原始一阶输出作为候选之一；(4) 通过最大化目标提示词的CLIP对齐度来选择最终图像。

Result: 在包含10种编辑类型、700个样本的PIE-Bench++评测上，该方法在当前所有一步更新算法中达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 通过将编辑强度权衡转化为候选选择问题，利用黎曼残差线搜索可有效平衡编辑的激进性与保守性，无需引入新的编辑模型即能显著提升一步扩散编辑的质量。

Abstract: One-step diffusion editors are fast because they avoid inversion and iterative optimization, but a single transport update must be aggressive enough to realize the target prompt and conservative enough to preserve the source image--and no fixed update strength satisfies both demands across edit types. We treat this tension as a post-hoc candidate-selection problem on top of energy-field transport rather than as a new editing model. Our proposed method, Riemannian Residual Line Search, first builds a stronger edit by estimating the local time curvature of the prompt-delta field and projecting the corrected direction back onto the update norm of the original first-order energy-field transport estimation. It then forms a small residual path from the source image to this strong edit, retains the original first-order output as one candidate, and picks the final image by maximizing target-prompt CLIP alignment. On a 700-sample PIE-Bench++ evaluation across 10 edit type IDs, our method achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance among current one-step update algorithms.

</details>


### [111] [Spherical-to-ERP Epipolar Rectification for Single-Axis Disparity in 360 Stereo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24847)
*Sahereh Obeidavi,Dieter Landes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将球面立体图像通过等距矩形投影（ERP）预处理后，直接送入已有的RAFT+EACS光流-视差流水线，从而将球面立体视差估计问题转化为标准的一维视差估计问题，实验验证了该方案的有效性和实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全向立体图像（球面或鱼眼）中，核线对应沿大圆路径弯曲，产生二维位移，无法在几何校正前将其视为单轴视差。需要一种简洁、可解释且高效的方法，将传统立体匹配管线扩展到360°成像场景。

Method: 采用球面到等距矩形投影（ERP）作为预处理步骤，将弯曲的核线拉直，恢复一维视差结构（左右配置为水平视差，上下配置为垂直视差）。随后，利用作者此前提出的RAFT+EACS（核线对齐通道选择）框架：先由RAFT估计稠密光流，再仅保留与基线方向对齐的光流分量作为视差。

Result: 在合成鱼眼立体数据集上的实验表明，该"球面→ERP→RAFT+EACS"流水线能够生成准确、平滑且结构一致的视差图，并达到实时推理速度。

Conclusion: 已有的ERP预处理策略可以与RAFT+EACS方法有效结合，为球面立体视觉提供一条实用、可解释且高效的视差估计路径，为将传统立体管线扩展至360°成像提供了简洁方案。

Abstract: Omnidirectional stereo images provide full-surround perception but violate the geometric assumptions of classical disparity estimation: in spherical or fisheye views, epipolar correspondences follow curved great-circle paths, producing two-dimensional displacements that cannot be treated as single-axis disparity before geometric rectification. In this work, we adopt a standard spherical-to-equirectangular (ERP) projection as a preprocessing step, which straightens epipolar curves and restores a one-dimensional disparity structure - horizontal for left-right rigs and vertical for top-bottom rigs. Building on our previously introduced RAFT + Epipolar-Aligned Channel Selection (EACS) framework, originally developed for rectilinear and ERP stereo, we examine whether the same modular pipeline remains accurate when the input originates from spherical stereo imagery. After ERP projection, dense optical flow from RAFT is reduced to disparity by retaining only the baseline-aligned flow component. Experiments on synthetic fisheye stereo datasets show that this spherical-to-ERP-to-RAFT+EACS pipeline produces accurate, smooth, and structurally consistent disparity maps at real-time speed. These findings confirm that established ERP preprocessing can be effectively combined with our earlier RAFT+EACS method to enable practical, interpretable, and efficient disparity estimation from spherical stereo, providing a straightforward pathway for extending conventional stereo pipelines to 360 imaging.

</details>


### [112] [IV-CoT: Implicit Visual Chain-of-Thought for Structure-Aware Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24849)
*Zixuan Li,Haokun Lin,Yicheng Xiao,Zhiwei Li,Xinyang Song,Zelong Zheng,Yong He,Heng Yao,Ke Ding,Chao Yu,Chuan Yuan,Qi Li,Zhenan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IV-CoT 提出隐式视觉思维链框架，将文生图的结构规划与外观渲染解耦为级联的潜在视觉推理过程，通过训练期草图监督引导结构查询，在单次前向传播中实现结构感知的图像生成，在 GenEval 和 T2I-CompBench 上取得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态大模型在文生图任务中虽生成质量高，但在结构感知的提示遵循（如对象计数、空间关系、属性绑定、布局）上表现不佳。作者将此归因于结构规划与外观渲染在单一条件流中的纠缠。

Method: 提出 IV-CoT 框架：将视觉条件查询分解为结构到语义的级联——结构查询先形成潜在视觉规划，语义查询再基于该规划渲染外观。引入仅训练期使用的草图监督，促使结构查询从草图中捕获结构信息，推理时无需草图提取或中间解码。整体在单次前向传播中完成隐式 CoT 推理。

Result: 在 GenEval 和 T2I-CompBench 基准上取得最优性能。可视化与分析表明，学习的结构查询和语义查询在结构感知生成中发挥互补作用。

Conclusion: 通过将结构规划与语义渲染解耦为隐式视觉思维链，IV-CoT 有效提升了 MLLM 在结构感知文生图任务上的表现，为条件图像生成中的结构化推理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Unified multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved strong text-to-image generation quality, but still struggle with structure-aware prompt following, where object counts, spatial relations, attribute bindings, and coarse layouts must be preserved. We attribute this limitation in part to the entanglement of structural planning and appearance rendering within a single conditioning stream. To address this issue, we propose Implicit Visual Chain-of-Thought (IV-CoT), a latent visual reasoning framework for query-conditioned image generation. IV-CoT decomposes the visual conditioning queries into a structural-to-semantic cascade, where structural queries first form a latent visual plan and semantic queries then render appearance conditioned on this plan. To guide the structural queries, we introduce training-only sketch supervision, which encourages them to capture structure from sketches without requiring sketch extraction or intermediate decoding at inference time. IV-CoT performs implicit CoT reasoning in a single forward pass and achieves superior results on GenEval and T2I-CompBench. Visualizations and analyses demonstrate that the learned structural and semantic queries play complementary roles in structure-aware generation.

</details>


### [113] [FLUX3D: High-Fidelity 3D Gaussian Generation with Diffusion-Aligned Sparse Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24874)
*Haorui Ji,Weizhe Liu,Hongdong Li,Hengkai Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FLUX3D 提出了一种可扩展的图像到3DGS生成框架，通过改进2D特征选择（DA-SLAT）和设计稀疏结构感知扩散框架（SMDiT + MARoPE），解决了稀疏体素表示中的表征瓶颈和跨模态对应瓶颈，在3DGS生成质量上显著超越现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于稀疏体素的图像到3DGS生成方法存在两个结构性瓶颈：一是使用面向语义抽象的判别式2D特征构建稀疏体素潜变量，抑制了重建线索（表征瓶颈）；二是标准扩散Transformer缺乏有效机制对齐密集2D图像token与稀疏3D体素潜变量（跨模态对应瓶颈）。

Method: 提出FLUX3D框架，包含两大核心设计：（1）重新审视2D特征选择，提出扩散对齐结构化潜变量（DA-SLAT），并采用decoder-only架构提升3DGS重建保真度；（2）设计稀疏结构感知扩散框架，集成稀疏结构多模态扩散Transformer（SMDiT）和模态感知旋转位置编码（MARoPE），实现几何无关的2D-3D对齐。

Result: 在广泛基准实验上，FLUX3D在外观保真度方面取得了显著提升，在生成高质量3DGS资产上全面超越所有SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 通过同时提升表征学习与跨模态对齐，FLUX3D成功克服了稀疏体素3DGS生成的两大瓶颈，为实现高保真图像到3D生成提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Sparse voxel representation has emerged as a scalable foundation for image-to-3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) generation, yet current methods struggle to preserve high-frequency visual details of input images due to two structural bottlenecks. First, they adopt discriminative 2D features optimized for semantic abstraction to construct sparse voxel latents, which suppress reconstructive cues and induce a representation bottleneck. Second, in the generation stage, standard diffusion transformers lack effective mechanisms to align dense 2D image tokens with sparse 3D voxel latents, resulting in a cross-modal correspondence bottleneck. To address these issues, we propose FLUX3D, a scalable image-to-3DGS framework that boosts both representation learning and cross-modal alignment during generation. We first revisit 2D feature selection for sparse-voxel-based 3D representation learning, propose Diffusion-Aligned Structured Latents (DA-SLAT) and couple it with a decoder-only architecture to improve 3DGS reconstruction fidelity. We also design a sparse-structure-aware diffusion framework, which integrates the Sparse-structure Multimodal Diffusion Transformer (SMDiT) and Modal-Aware Rotary Positional Embedding (MARoPE) to achieve geometry-agnostic 2D-3D alignment. Extensive benchmark experiments demonstrate that FLUX3D yields substantial improvements in appearance fidelity and significantly outperforms all state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods in generating high-quality 3DGS assets.

</details>


### [114] [FLAT: Feedforward Latent Triangle Splatting for Geometrically Accurate Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24876)
*Orest Kupyn,Goutam Bhat,Philipp Henzler,Fabian Manhardt,Christian Rupprecht,Federico Tombari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FLAT 首次实现从视频扩散模型的压缩潜变量中直接解码出三角形基元（triangle splats），通过射线中心旋转参数化和乘积窗口函数解决了平面基元梯度优化难题，在保持视觉质量的同时显著提升几何精度，并可经轻量测试时优化转换为游戏引擎可用的实时渲染资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈潜场景解码器多输出体积式3D高斯，缺乏良好定义的表面，难以用于仿真或标准图形管线。作者希望探索是否可将压缩视频扩散潜变量直接映射为显式表面基元，获得更接近可用几何资产的表示。

Method: 提出FLAT方法，核心包括：(1) 面向射线中心的旋转参数化用于三角形回归，降低对基元朝向的敏感性；(2) 新型乘积窗口函数，改善可微三角形渲染中的梯度流动。此外引入轻量测试时优化步骤，将预测的三角形面片集合转换为完全不透明的游戏引擎就绪表示。

Result: 在标准基准上，FLAT相比最先进前馈基线取得显著更优的几何精度，同时保持有竞争力的视觉质量。首次在相同训练设置下系统分析了3DGS、2DGS及三角形splatting变体在前馈场景生成中的表示权衡。

Conclusion: 从视频扩散潜变量直接解码显式表面基元是可行的，FLAT通过改进参数化与梯度估计成功克服了平面基元优化的困难，为前馈场景生成中几何表示的选择提供了系统性参考，并展示了向游戏引擎资产转化的实用路径。

Abstract: Generating explorable 3D scenes from a single image requires strong generative priors and accurate geometric representations suitable for downstream use. Current video diffusion models offer high-quality generation and implicitly encode multi-view geometric structure in latent space. However, existing feedforward latent scene decoders typically output volumetric 3D Gaussians that lack a well-defined surface, limiting their use in simulation or standard graphics pipelines. This motivates decoding surface-aligned primitives that are not only renderable but also closer to explicit geometric assets. We ask whether compressed video diffusion latents can be mapped directly to explicit surface primitives in a single pass. To this end, we introduce FLAT and, for the first time, show that triangle splats can be decoded directly from video diffusion latents. Compared with decoding 3D Gaussians, predicting flat primitives is notoriously more challenging due to high sensitivity to primitive orientations, oftentimes leading to poor gradient flow. FLAT solves with two key ingredients: a ray-centered rotation parameterization for triangle regression and a novel product window function that improves gradient flow during differentiable triangle rendering. On standard benchmarks, FLAT achieves significantly better geometric accuracy while maintaining competitive visual quality compared to state-of-the-art feedforward baselines. We further show that a lightweight test-time refinement step converts the predicted triangle soup into a fully opaque, game-engine-ready representation that supports real-time rendering. By evaluating 3DGS, 2DGS, and triangle splatting variants under an identical training setup, we provide the first systematic analysis of representation tradeoffs in feedforward scene generation. The project page is available at https://flat-splat.github.io

</details>


### [115] [BenchX: Benchmarking AI Models for Cancer Detection and Localization with Demographic and Protocol Biases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24883)
*Qi Chen,Wenxuan Li,Pedro R. A. S. Bassi,Xinze Zhou,Jakob Wasserthal,Ibrahim Ethem Hamamci,Sezgin Er,Ashwin Kumar,Yiwen Ye,Yuhan Wang,Yuyin Zhou,Akshay S. Chaudhari,Curtis Langlotz,Kang Wang,Yang Yang,Alan L. Yuille,Zongwei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个包含85,355张CT扫描的大规模开放基准，系统评估了12种肿瘤检测AI模型在肿瘤大小、位置、患者亚群和成像协议等维度上的表现，揭示了当前AI模型在罕见/代表性不足亚群（如年轻非裔美国女性）中性能显著下降的问题，并利用大语言模型提取临床亚群信息，为构建更鲁棒的医学影像AI奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医学影像AI模型虽然在平均准确率上表现优异，但在真实临床环境中常因患者人口统计学差异和成像协议变化而表现不一致。现有的评估方法缺乏对亚群层面性能的系统性量化，且罕见病例的标注数据难以收集。研究旨在通过大规模基准测试，量化并揭示这些不一致性，推动更可靠、更公平的AI模型开发。

Method: 1. 构建了一个包含85,355张CT扫描的大规模开放基准数据集；2. 利用大语言模型（LLMs）从临床数据中自动提取并整理亚群信息（如人口统计学、成像协议），使分析可扩展且可复现；3. 在肿瘤大小、位置、患者亚群（年龄、性别、种族）和成像协议（如不同对比期相）等多个维度上，系统评估了12种最先进的肿瘤检测AI模型。

Result: 基准测试结果表明，当前针对平均准确率优化的SOTA AI模型在罕见或代表性不足的亚群（如年轻、非裔美国女性）中表现显著下降，暴露了模型在不同亚群间的性能不一致性问题。

Conclusion: 该基准为构建更可靠、更鲁棒的肿瘤检测AI模型提供了基础，强调了在医学影像和计算机视觉领域进行严格的亚群层面评估的必要性。研究倡导在模型开发中关注亚群公平性和泛化能力，而非仅追求整体平均性能。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) has achieved remarkable success in medical imaging, but it is widely recognized that these models often perform inconsistently across real-world clinical settings. Such inconsistencies occur when patient demographics and imaging protocols vary, for example, in detecting small tumors, analyzing scans from different contrast phases, or evaluating patients of different ages or sexes. To quantify these inconsistencies, we develop a large-scale, open benchmark of 85,355 CT scans that systematically evaluates 12 tumor-detection AI models across tumor size, location, patient subgroup, and imaging protocol. We leverage large language models (LLMs) to extract and organize subgroup information from clinical data, which makes the analysis both scalable and reproducible. Our benchmark reveals that current state-of-the-art AI models, optimized for average accuracy, perform poorly in rare or underrepresented subgroups, such as young, female African Americans. However, collecting sufficient annotated data for these rare cases is often impractical. The benchmark provides a foundation for building more reliable and robust AI models for tumor detection and highlighting the need for rigorous, subgroup-level evaluation in medical imaging and computer vision. Datasets, code

</details>


### [116] [DiffusionBench: On Holistic Evaluation of Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24888)
*Xingjian Leng,Jaskirat Singh,Zhanhao Liang,Ethan Smith,Martin Bell,Aninda Saha,Yuhui Yuan,Liang Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NanoGen 是一个统一的 DiT 训练评估框架，使文本到图像训练成本与 ImageNet 相当。作者发现 ImageNet 与 T2I 上的方法排名相关性极低（Pearson r: -0.377 至 -0.580），表明仅凭 ImageNet FID 无法反映真实进展，因此提出 DiffusionBench 作为更全面的评估基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 DiT 研究过度依赖 ImageNet 类条件生成作为单一评测设置，方法在 FID 等指标上的提升是否真正反映生成建模的实质进步存疑。文本到图像（T2I）生成被视为成本过高或不便于训练评估而被广泛跳过。作者认为这一认知已不再成立，需要打破这一现状。

Method: 提出 NanoGen，一个统一的 DiT 训练与评估框架，仅需 12 行配置变更即可从 ImageNet 切换到 T2I 训练。该框架支持 RAE、VAE、像素空间和 MeanFlow 等多种扩散方法，覆盖 ImageNet 与 T2I 两种设定。作者使用 NanoGen 训练了 21 个潜在扩散模型，并通过 Pearson 相关系数量化两种任务上的方法排名一致性。

Result: NanoGen 在 ImageNet 上匹配当前最优 DiT 基线，T2I 训练计算量与 ImageNet 相当。21 个模型的对比显示，ImageNet 与 T2I 之间的方法排名无强相关性：三项指标的 Pearson 相关系数在 -0.377 到 -0.580 之间（呈中度负相关）。

Conclusion: ImageNet 类条件 FID 的改进不必然对应 T2I 上的提升，DiT 研究必须在两类任务上同时评测。作者汇总 ImageNet 与 T2I 结果形成 DiffusionBench，建议以此替代单一 ImageNet 评估，使方法排名更可能反映广泛的真实进展。

Abstract: Diffusion transformer (DiT) research on image generation has converged to a single evaluation setup: class-conditional generation on ImageNet. While methods improve the FID and related metrics, it is increasingly unclear whether they reflect real progress in generative modeling. The natural alternative, i.e., text-to-image (T2I) generation, is perceived as too costly or inconvenient to train and evaluate and is often skipped. We argue that this perception no longer holds. We introduce NanoGen, a unified DiT training and evaluation framework. NanoGen matches state-of-the-art DiT baselines on ImageNet and, with 12 lines of configuration change, also trains competitive text-to-image models. It currently supports RAE, VAE, pixel-space, and MeanFlow diffusion methods under both ImageNet and T2I setups. Under NanoGen, training T2I requires comparable compute to ImageNet. After training 21 latent diffusion models with NanoGen, we observe that method ranking shows no strong correlation between ImageNet and T2I generation: Pearson correlation is between -0.377 and -0.580 across three metrics. This suggests that a method which improves class-conditional ImageNet FID may show no corresponding improvement on T2I, clearly indicating the necessity of evaluating DiTs on both tasks. To this end, we summarize ImageNet and text-to-image results, which yields DiffusionBench, a holistic benchmark for DiT research. We recommend reporting DiffusionBench in place of ImageNet alone: methods that improve DiffusionBench are more likely to reflect broader progress.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [EXPO-SQL: Execution-based Clause-level Policy Optimization for Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23693)
*Jaehoon Lee,CheolWon Na,Suyoung Bae,Jin-Seop Lee,Jihyung Lee,YunSeok Choi,Jee-Hyong Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EXPO-SQL 提出了一种基于执行的子句级策略优化方法，通过细粒度的子句级奖励替代传统的查询级统一奖励，为Text-to-SQL任务提供更强的学习信号，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的Text-to-SQL方法对所有SQL子句（无论正确与否）分配统一的查询级奖励，这种粗粒度设计导致正确子句生成缺乏足够的学习信号，限制了模型性能。

Method: 提出EXPO-SQL框架：(1) 通过分析执行结果（包括错误消息和子句级增量执行）来定位错误子句；(2) 基于错误定位为每个子句分配细粒度的子句级奖励；(3) 利用子句级奖励进行策略优化训练。

Result: 在广泛使用的Text-to-SQL基准测试上，EXPO-SQL显著优于现有的监督微调、提示工程以及基于强化学习的方法。

Conclusion: 细粒度的子句级学习能有效解决RL-based Text-to-SQL中奖励信号不足的问题，通过子句级奖励提供更精细的监督，大幅提升SQL生成质量。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL enables users to query databases using natural language by generating executable SQL queries. Recent methods have increasingly adopted Large Language Models based reinforcement learning (RL) to leverage execution feedback for training. However, existing RL methods assign uniform query-level rewards to all clauses in a SQL query, treating correct and incorrect clauses equally. This coarse-grained reward design leads to insufficient learning signals for correct SQL generation. To address this issue, we propose EXPO-SQL (EXecution-based clause-level Policy Optimization for Text-to-SQL) which provides fine-grained supervision through clause-level rewards. To assign clause-level rewards, our method identifies erroneous clauses by analyzing execution results, including error messages and clause-wise incremental execution. Experiments on widely-used Text-to-SQL benchmarks demonstrate that EXPO-SQL significantly outperforms existing supervised fine-tuning, prompting, and RL-based methods through fine-grained clause-level learning. Our code is available at https://github. com/jhn25/EXPO-SQL.

</details>


### [118] [ModTGCN: Modularity-aware Graph Neural Networks for Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23694)
*Rajarshi Misra,Aditya Sharma,Vinti Agarwal,Hari Om Aggrawal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ModTGCN 提出一种模块度感知的图神经网络，通过联合优化交叉熵和模块度辅助目标，在文本分类中增强类间一致性的文档社区结构，同时解耦异构图以实现2-10倍训练加速，在低同配性数据集上表现尤为突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的文本分类模型仅依赖局部邻域聚合，忽略了语义文档图中天然的全局社区结构（类内紧密、类间稀疏），导致类别边界模糊和过平滑问题。引入模块度优化可显式建模这种社区聚类特性。

Method: 提出 ModTGCN：(1) 联合优化交叉熵损失与基于模块度的辅助损失，模块度在transformer嵌入构建的文档-文档相似度图上计算；(2) 将异构图解耦为独立的文档-词和词-词子图，加速训练2-10倍；(3) 研究图构建策略、标签感知边重加权及模块度优化的监督方式选择。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上取得一致性能提升，尤其在Ohsumed和20NG等复杂度高、同配性低的数据集上改进更为显著。

Conclusion: ModTGCN 通过模块度驱动的社区结构建模有效缓解了过平滑问题，解耦图设计兼顾了效率，验证了全局社区信息对图文本分类的重要价值。

Abstract: Graph-based text classification models typically rely on local neighborhood aggregation and overlook global community structure, despite semantic document graphs exhibiting strong class-consistent clustering. Ignoring this can blur class boundaries and lead to over-smoothing. We propose ModTGCN, a modularity-aware graph neural network for text classification that jointly optimizes cross-entropy and a modularity-based auxiliary objective to promote class-coherent document communities while preserving discriminative representations. The modularity term is computed on a document-document similarity graph derived from transformer embeddings (pretrained or fine-tuned). To improve scalability, we decouple the original heterogeneous TextGCN graph into separate document-word and word-word components, achieving 2x-10x faster training. We further study graph construction strategies, label-aware edge reweighting, and supervision choices for modularity optimization. Experiments on five benchmarks show consistent gains, with larger improvements on complex, low homophily datasets such as Ohsumed and 20NG.

</details>


### [119] [Quantifying Prior Dominance in RAG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23695)
*Barak Or*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出归一化上下文利用（NCU）指标，通过对比零样本、Oracle和对抗三种条件下的token对数概率，严格量化RAG系统中的上下文信息增益。研究发现：在严格事实提取任务中，传统缩放定律呈现极端边际收益递减，小型语言模型（SLM）可媲美甚至超越大模型；同时，"先验主导"现象与模型规模和商业对齐相关，商业API在近半数对抗冲突中无视外部证据，并出现系统性置信度崩溃（负迁移）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RAG评估依赖离散启发式方法，存在"认知盲区"——无法区分模型是从外部上下文提取信息还是依赖参数化记忆进行复述，亟需一种能严格量化上下文信息增益的连续度量方法。

Method: 提出归一化上下文利用（NCU）指标，利用零样本（无上下文）、Oracle（黄金上下文）和对抗（冲突上下文）三种条件下的连续token对数概率，严格测量上下文信息增益。在1.5B至72B参数规模的多种架构及商业API上进行评估，聚焦于严格事实提取任务（不启用思维链推理）。

Result: （1）传统缩放定律在严格事实提取中呈现极端边际收益递减，SLM可匹配或超越大模型；（2）"先验主导"现象与模型规模和商业对齐呈正相关；（3）商业API在近半数对抗冲突中无视显式外部证据，且在参数先验被矛盾时频繁出现系统性置信度崩溃（负迁移）。

Conclusion: SLM在严格提取工作流中具有结构性的认知优势和更优的上下文依从性，挑战了"越大越好"的传统观念，为RAG系统在特定场景下选择高效小模型提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) grounds Large Language Models in external knowledge, yet current evaluations rely on discrete heuristics that suffer from ''epistemic blindness'' - failing to distinguish genuine contextual information extraction from parametric memory recall. To address this, we introduce the Normalized Context Utilization (NCU) metric, leveraging continuous token log-probabilities across zero-shot, oracle, and adversarial conditions to strictly quantify contextual information gain. Evaluating architectures ranging from 1.5B to 72B parameters alongside a proprietary commercial API reveals that for strict factual extraction (without Chain-of-Thought reasoning), traditional scaling laws exhibit extreme diminishing returns: highly efficient Small Language Models (SLMs) match or outperform high-capacity architectures. Furthermore, we demonstrate that ``Prior Dominance'' correlates with model scale and proprietary alignments. The evaluated commercial API not only overrode explicit external evidence in nearly half of adversarial conflicts, but also frequently suffered from systemic confidence collapse (Negative Transfer) when its parametric priors were contradicted. Our findings highlight the structural epistemic advantage and superior contextual adherence of SLMs in strict extraction workflows.

</details>


### [120] [Self-Recognition Finetuning can Prevent and Reverse Emergent Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23700)
*Arush Tagade,Shaoheng Zhou,Jiaxin Wen,Shi Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 涌现性失对齐(EM)并非模型习得有害内容，而是对齐角色被破坏所致。本文提出通过自生成文本识别微调(SGTR)来强化角色，发现其在预防EM方面尤为有效，并将EM重新定义为对齐角色的失稳而非一致的对齐失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将涌现性失对齐(EM)与失对齐人格向量和邪恶角色特质相关联，暗示EM的运行机制是破坏模型的对齐角色，而非直接学习有害内容。基于此，作者希望探索一种面向角色本身的针对性防御策略，以区别于现有的训练中防御方法。

Method: 作者在三个模型(GPT-4.1、Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct、Seed-OSS-36B-Instruct)和多个EM数据集上进行两阶段微调实验：(1)将自生成文本识别微调(SGTR)与良性微调基线(领域正确数据、通用知识、单词计数)进行比较；(2)在逆转(reversal)和预防(prevention)两种设置下评估防御效果；(3)通过分析身份自报多样性、破坏自识别能力、移除身份承载系统提示等实验，进一步验证EM与默认角色之间的关系。

Result: 在逆转设置下，所有干预方法在恢复EM所退化的能力后均产生相当的逆转效果。在预防设置下，仅SGTR微调能够持续降低失对齐且不加剧任何单一指标，表明角色强化是预防的关键驱动因素。此外，EM微调会诱导模型身份自报的多样性增加，人为破坏自识别能力会加剧EM失对齐，移除身份承载系统提示则大幅削弱EM微调的影响。

Conclusion: 涌现性失对齐不应被理解为模型采纳了一致的失对齐人格，而应被重新定义为模型对齐角色的失稳。SGTR微调作为一种面向角色的针对性干预，尤其在预防EM方面展现出独特优势，为理解与防御EM提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Emergent misalignment (EM) has been linked to the activation of misaligned persona vectors and evil character traits, suggesting that EM operates through disruption of the model's aligned character rather than direct learning of harmful content. Motivated by this connection, we study self-generated text recognition (SGTR) finetuning as a character-targeted intervention that is distinct from existing in-training defenses. We conduct two-stage finetuning experiments across three models (GPT-4.1, Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct, Seed-OSS-36B-Instruct) and multiple EM datasets to compare SGTR finetuning against benign finetuning baselines (correct domain-specific data, general knowledge, and word counting) to find it an effective defense in both reversal and prevention settings. We find that all interventions produce comparable EM reversal, but only when restoring capabilities that EM had degraded. For prevention, only SGTR finetuning consistently reduces misalignment without exacerbating any individual metric, suggesting that character fortification specifically drives prevention. We provide further evidence for EM's relation to the LLM's default character by showing that EM finetuning induces diversity into the LLM's identity self-reports, artificially corrupting self-recognition exacerbates misalignment caused by EM finetuning, and that removing the model's identity-bearing system prompt substantially reduces the effect of EM finetuning. Together, these findings reframe EM not as the adoption of a coherent misaligned persona but as the destabilization of aligned character.

</details>


### [121] [Evaluating LLM Usage for Efficient and Explainable Numerical and Classified Implicit Sentiment Analysis of Product Desirability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23701)
*Sherri Weitl-Harms,John Hastings*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种使用大语言模型（LLMs）从定性产品反馈中量化产品满意度的可扩展框架，在两个PDT数据集上实现了高达0.97的Pearson相关系数和94%的分类准确率，且GPT-4o-mini以低94%的成本达到与更大模型相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定性产品反馈蕴含丰富的用户情感体验，但其隐含的情感难以量化。传统基于词典或Transformer的方法效果不佳，需要一种可扩展、可解释的解决方案来从非结构化文本中可靠地测量产品满意度。

Method: 基于两个PDT数据集（ZORQ和CARMA，共106个受访者术语分组及人工标注金标准），采用零样本连续数值情感打分和分类情感标注，使用LLMs直接从定性回复中生成情感分数，并辅以模型置信度评分和可解释AI（xAI）的人类可读解释。

Result: LLMs在两个数据集上分别取得最高0.97的Pearson相关系数和94%的分类准确率；对多种数据格式表现稳健且置信度高；GPT-4o-mini以低94%的成本达到与更大模型相当的性能；基于词典和Transformer的基线方法未产生统计显著的结果。

Conclusion: 将PDT调查工具与成本高效的LLM情感分析相结合，能够提供丰富的情感评分（数值和分类）以及高层次用户印象，为产品开发改进和面向目标受众的营销策略提供有力支持，该框架兼具可扩展性、可解释性和实用性。

Abstract: Qualitative product feedback can reveal nuanced user experiences, but its implicit sentiment is difficult to measure. This paper presents a scalable and interpretable framework that uses large language models (LLMs) to quantify product desirability from such data. Using two Product Desirability Toolkit (PDT) datasets from ZORQ and CARMA comprising 106 respondent term groupings with gold-standard human annotation, zero-shot continuous numerical sentiment scoring and categorical sentiment classification are evaluated without relying on explicit review scores. Across the datasets, LLMs generated numerical sentiment scores directly from qualitative responses and closely matched expert labels, achieving Pearson correlations up to 0.97 and classification accuracy up to 94%. LLMs maintained robustness even when handling data presented in multiple forms and consistently expressed high confidence. In contrast, lexicon-based and transformer baselines did not produce statistically significant results. Among the models tested, GPT-4o-mini achieved performance comparable to larger models at 94% lower cost, supporting scalable deployment.
  The framework also incorporates model confidence ratings and human-readable rationale explanations (xAI), improving interpretability, transparency, and trust while supporting practical use in product satisfaction assessment. In general, using the PDT tool as a survey method along with a cost efficient LLM for sentiment analysis has the potential to provide for product evaluation with results that are rich in terms of sentiment scores (both numerical and classified sentiment) and in terms of the high-level user impressions of the product that can be used to identify ideas for product development and improvement, as well as marketing ideas for target audiences.

</details>


### [122] [Ground Then Rank: Revisiting Knowledge-Based VQA with Training-Free Entity Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23881)
*Qian Ma,Qiong Wu,Zhengyi Zhou,Yao Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种训练无关的"先识别后回答"(IBA)框架用于知识型视觉问答(KB-VQA)，将实体识别与证据排序解耦：先让多模态大模型从候选实体名中选择高置信度实体，再通过现成文本排序器选取证据，在Encyclopedic-VQA和InfoSeek上一致优于微调的多模态重排序基线，同时降低了训练与推理复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态检索增强生成(MM-RAG)方法将实体判别和段落级证据排序紧耦合到单一重排序阶段，成本高且泛化性受限。KB-VQA中实体级和事实级 grounding 是关键瓶颈：MLLMs在开放命名中常失败，但面对候选实体集时能更好识别正确实体。

Method: 提出Identify-Before-Answer (IBA)框架：(1) 解耦实体识别与段落级重排序；(2) 利用MLLM仅基于候选实体名称选择高置信度实体；(3) 使用现成的纯文本重排序器进行证据选择；(4) 整个流程无需训练。

Result: 在Encyclopedic-VQA和InfoSeek数据集上，IBA方法一致优于微调的多模态重排序基线，同时降低了训练和推理复杂度。消融分析表明改进来自两方面：更优的实体识别，以及确定正确实体后能选择更具信息量的证据。

Conclusion: 通过将实体识别与证据排序解耦，IBA以简洁、无需训练的方式解决了KB-VQA中实体级和证据级grounding的双重瓶颈，性能超越微调基线且计算开销更低，具有良好的实用性与可复现性。

Abstract: Knowledge-Based Visual Question Answering (KB-VQA) requires grounding visual queries to external knowledge beyond directly observable content in images. While recent multi modal large language models (MLLMs) show strong perceptual abilities, they struggle on KB-VQA tasks requiring groundings from both fine-grained entity and evidence levels. Most existing multi-modal retrieval augmented generation (MM-RAG) methods tightly couple entity discrimination and section-level evidence ranking into a single re-ranking stage, leading to high cost and limited generalization. In this work, we revisit existing MM-RAG solutions from a workflow perspective and argue both entity-level and fact-level groundings are key bottlenecks. We observe that although MLLMs often fail under open-ended entity naming, they can better identify the correct entity when selecting from a small set of candidate names. Based on this insight, we propose a simple and training-free identify-before-answer IBA framework that decouples entity identification from section-level re-ranking. Our approach prompts an MLLM to select high-confidence entities using only candidate names, followed by an off-the-shelf textual re-ranker for evidence selection. Experiments on Encyclopedic-VQA and InfoSeek show that our method consistently outperforms fine-tuned multi-modal re-ranking baselines while reducing training and inference complexity. Additional analyses reveal that the improvements arise not only from better entity identification, but also from selecting more informative evidence once correct entity is fixed. Our implementation is made public to ease reproducibility.

</details>


### [123] [One Year Later...The Harms Persist, But So Do We!](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23884)
*Annika Marie Schoene,Cansu Canca,Gautham Vijay Kumar,Anson Antony*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了六种主流大语言模型在16种DSM-5精神疾病条件下面对对抗攻击时的安全防护表现，发现仅自杀和自伤防护可靠，而进食障碍、物质使用障碍、重度抑郁等场景失败率高达100%，呼吁建立按临床条件细分的伤害分类体系与针对性防护机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型正越来越多地被用于心理健康对话，但其安全防护在不同临床条件下表现不一致且不充分。现有安全措施缺乏针对特定精神疾病的细粒度危害分类和评估框架，导致模型在面对特定临床场景时防护失效，对脆弱人群构成显著风险。

Method: 研究选取六种商用大语言模型，覆盖16种DSM-5定义的精神疾病条件，采用四种对抗攻击变体进行测试。引入了一个包含八个维度的伤害分类体系（eight-dimension harm taxonomy）和多维度评估框架，系统性地评估模型在不同临床条件下的安全防护失效情况。

Result: 防护措施仅在自杀和自伤相关场景中可靠生效；而在进食障碍、物质使用障碍和重度抑郁障碍等条件下，防护失败率高达100%。不同临床条件的防护表现差异显著，安全措施具有高度选择性而非全面覆盖。

Conclusion: 大语言模型的伦理化设计与部署要求在临床条件层面明确伤害类别，并据此实施针对性安全防护。在当前防护机制完善之前，这些模型对脆弱人群构成重大风险，尤其在教育场景中的日益普及更令人担忧。

Abstract: General-purpose large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for mental health-related conversations, yet safety safeguards remain inadequate and inconsistent across clinical conditions. This study evaluates six proprietary LLMs across 16 DSM-5 conditions using four adversarial attack variants, introducing an eight-dimension harm taxonomy and a multi-dimensional evaluation framework. Results show that safeguards hold reliably only for suicide and self-harm, while conditions such as eating disorders, substance use disorder, and major depressive disorder exhibit failure rates of up to 100%. We argue that ethical design and deployment of these LLMs demand clearly defined harm categories across clinical conditions and implementation of safeguards accordingly. Until such safeguards are in place, these models pose significant risks to vulnerable populations, making their growing integration into educational settings a particularly concerning.

</details>


### [124] [Do LLM Attribution Metrics Transfer? Auditing Retrieval-Augmented Generation Evaluation Across Datasets and Constructs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23915)
*Tianyu Ding,Aditya Nannapaneni,Juan Pablo De la Cruz Weinstein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 对八种自动归因评分器的审计发现，没有一种评分器能在生成答案归因的多个数据集上保持稳定表现：指标排名在不同数据集间发生反转（Kendall tau = -0.64），最佳评分器从短文本上的NLI模型（AUROC 0.90）变为长文本上的BERTScore（0.91），这种不稳定性带来了具体的决策代价。基于提示的LLM评判器避免了崩溃但并未消除验证负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践领域常将LLM检索增强生成中的自动归因指标视为可互换使用，但缺乏对跨数据集、跨评估构念一致性的系统审计。研究旨在揭示这种互换假设是否成立，以及指标选择的实际决策代价。

Method: 审计八种自动评分器（词法、嵌入、BERTScore基线，以及基于蕴涵/事实性训练的模型如NLI、MiniCheck），在三个评估构念（来源/主题性、生成答案归因、事实验证蕴涵）上进行测试。核心分析聚焦生成答案归因构念，使用AttributionBench的四个源数据集（n=1,610）和独立HAGRID（n=2,150），通过留一数据集交叉验证、Kendall tau排名相关性、以及"最佳均值"规则的留一数据集遗憾值来量化不稳定性。

Result: 在生成答案归因构念中，无评分器能在所有数据集的95%置信区间内保持最佳：AttributedQA与LFQA间的指标排名显著负相关（Kendall tau = -0.64, p=0.031）；NLI评分器在短文本AttributedQA上最优（AUROC 0.90），但在长文本LFQA上降至随机水平（AUROC 0.53），而BERTScore在此场景最优（0.91），该翻转非长度或截断伪影。采用"最佳均值"规则选择评分器导致留一数据集平均遗憾值为0.172 AUROC，比固定单一评分器更差。基于提示的LLM评判器虽避免了自动评分器的机会水平崩溃，但并非一致最优，成本约高100倍且非确定性。

Conclusion: 自动归因评分器不可互换，指标选择必须在目标数据集上验证而非从其他数据集外推。"最佳均值"启发式规则在实践中可能适得其反。LLM评判器虽缓解了崩溃问题，但将验证负担转移到了成本、一致性和可靠性维度，而非消除。

Abstract: Practice often treats automatic metrics for attribution in LLM retrieval-augmented generation as interchangeable. We audit eight automatic scorers -- lexical, embedding, and BERTScore baselines alongside entailment/grounding-trained models (clean and FEVER NLI, the checker MiniCheck) -- across three evaluation constructs (provenance/topicality, generated-answer attribution, and fact-check entailment), asking whether any scorer transfers: stays within the 95% confidence interval of the best audited scorer on every dataset of a multi-dataset construct. In the construct with the most multi-dataset human-labeled coverage -- generated-answer attribution (AttributionBench's four source datasets, n = 1,610, with independent HAGRID, n = 2,150) -- none does: the per-dataset metric rankings invert (Kendall tau = -0.64, p = 0.031 on AttributedQA vs. LFQA), and an off-the-shelf NLI scorer that is best on short-claim AttributedQA (AUROC 0.90) collapses to AUROC 0.53 (chance) on long-form LFQA, where BERTScore wins (0.91); the flip is not a length or truncation artifact. This instability has a concrete decision cost: a naive "best-on-average" rule for choosing an evaluator fails leave-one-dataset-out (mean held-out regret 0.172 AUROC, worse than fixing one scorer), so metric choice must be validated on the target dataset rather than learned from others. A prompt-based LLM judge avoids the chance-level collapses the automatic scorers suffer (no LFQA collapse) but is not uniformly best, ~100x costlier, and non-deterministic -- relocating, not removing, the validation burden.

</details>


### [125] [When Retrieval Metrics Mislead: Measuring Policy Signal in Long-Horizon Tool-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23937)
*Tianyu Ding,Juan Pablo De la Cruz Weinstein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在tau-bench环境中检验了精确匹配检索召回率作为下游决策模型策略上下文质量代理指标的可靠性。实验表明，尽管精确匹配召回率很低（仅7%），但使用检索到的策略条款时，分类器的宏观F1分数仅比使用金标准条款低0.02，差距不显著，说明精确匹配召回率会低估检索策略的实际下游效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确匹配检索召回率常被用作评估检索器是否为下游决策模型提供有用策略上下文的代理指标，但该代理指标的有效性尚未经过严格验证。作者旨在检验这一代理指标在tau-bench环境中的可靠性，探究低召回率是否必然意味着下游分类性能的显著下降。

Method: 使用Qwen2.5-3B和7B模型作为分类器，在tau-bench上进行动作前策略分类任务。首先在金标准策略条件下，对比结构化状态与原始轨迹的输入表示效果。随后将基准指定的策略条款替换为从决策时上下文中检索到的排名第一的条款，比较检索条款与金标准条款下的分类性能，并设置不匹配策略和无策略两种对照条件。

Result: 1）金标准策略条件下，紧凑结构化状态表示比原始轨迹的宏观F1提升0.13-0.17；2）航空场景中仅7%的状态能在排名第一的位置精确匹配到正确条款，但3B分类器在检索条款下宏观F1为0.58，与金标准条款的0.60相比仅差-0.02（95% CI [-0.23, +0.21]），而两个对照组分别为0.32和0.21；3）未检测到检索与金标准条款间的显著宏观F1差异，但置信区间过宽无法确立非劣效性；4）第二种检索器和7B模型下呈现相同定性模式，但不同微调配置间存在差异。

Conclusion: 精确匹配条款召回率可能低估下游策略效用，建议在评估检索器时采用"分类闭环中检索策略"的评估方式，而非仅依赖召回率指标。

Abstract: Exact-match retrieval recall is often used as a proxy for whether a retriever supplies useful policy context to a downstream decision model. We test this proxy for pre-action policy classification in tau-bench using Qwen2.5-3B/7B classifiers. Under gold-policy conditioning, a compact structured state improves macro-F1 over raw trajectories by 0.13-0.17 after tuning. We then replace the benchmark-designated policy clause with the top-ranked clause retrieved from decision-time context. Although the exact governing clause is retrieved at rank 1 for only 7% of airline states, the primary 3B classifier obtains macro-F1 0.58 with retrieved clauses versus 0.60 with gold clauses (Delta=-0.02, task-cluster 95% CI [-0.23,+0.21]); mismatched-policy and no-policy controls score 0.32 and 0.21. We do not detect a macro-F1 difference between retrieved and gold clauses in this configuration, although the interval remains too wide to establish non-inferiority. The same qualitative pattern appears with a second retriever and at 7B, while varying across fine-tuning configurations. These results indicate that exact-match clause recall can underestimate downstream policy utility in this benchmark setting, motivating evaluation with retrieved policies in the classification loop rather than recall alone.

</details>


### [126] [QuechuaTok: Morphological Boundary Accuracy as a Necessary Metric for Tokenizer Evaluation in Agglutinative Low-Resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23943)
*Maria Contreras*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了面向南克丘亚语（低资源黏着语）的评测基准 QuechuaTok，对比四种分词策略（BPE、Unigram LM、WordPiece、PRPE），揭示生育率（fertility rate）在评估黏着语分词质量时存在严重缺陷，并提出形态边界准确率（MorphAcc）作为补充指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准分词评估指标（如 fertility rate）无法捕捉黏着语的形态学正确性，而低资源黏着语的分词评估缺乏系统性基准。南克丘亚语有 800-1000 万使用者但资源匮乏，亟需专门的分词评测框架。

Method: 基于 20 万句语料，以 SQUOIA 有限状态形态分析器（Rios, 2016）为银标准，系统对比四种分词策略：BPE、Unigram LM、WordPiece 和形态感知的 PRPE 分词器。采用三项指标：fertility rate、OOV rate、形态边界准确率（MorphAcc）。

Result: BPE 在 16k 词表下获得最低 fertility rate（1.636），但 MorphAcc 仅 6.67%；PRPE 获得最高 MorphAcc（83.33%），证明 fertility rate 单独不足以评估黏着语分词器。

Conclusion: 对于黏着语，fertility rate 低可能只是表面词形记忆的结果，而非真正捕获了形态结构。形态边界准确率应作为黏着语分词评估的关键补充指标。代码与模型已公开。

Abstract: Tokenization is a foundational step in NLP pipelines, yet standard evaluation metrics such as fertility rate fail to capture morphological correctness for agglutinative languages. We present QuechuaTok, a systematic benchmark comparing four tokenization strategies - BPE, Unigram LM, WordPiece, and a morphology-aware PRPE tokenizer - for Southern Quechua (quz), a low-resource agglutinative language spoken by 8-10 million people in South America. Using a 200k-sentence corpus and the SQUOIA finite-state morphological analyzer (Rios, 2016) as silver standard, we evaluate three metrics: fertility rate, OOV rate, and morphological boundary accuracy (MorphAcc). Our results show that BPE achieves the lowest fertility rate (1.636 at 16k vocab) by memorizing surface word forms, while achieving only 6.67% MorphAcc. PRPE achieves 83.33% MorphAcc - the highest of all systems - demonstrating that fertility rate alone is insufficient to evaluate tokenizers for agglutinative languages. All code and models are publicly available at kaggle.com/code/macmaky/quechuatok

</details>


### [127] [Layer-wise Probing of wav2vec 2.0 and Whisper for Consonant Cluster Reduction in African American English](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23948)
*Hamid Mojarad,Kevin Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过层级探测方法，考察wav2vec2-base和Whisper-small两种语音模型对非裔美国英语（AAE）中辅音簇简化（CCR）现象的内部表征。结果表明，两种模型不仅能高精度区分简化形式与完整形式，而且简化音段仍保留底层塞音的线索，说明CCR被编码为结构化的梯度音系变异，而非简单的音段删除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音模型内部表征的语言学可解释性研究日益增多，但AAE中的辅音簇简化（CCR）——一种广泛存在的音系过程，同时也是自动语音识别（ASR）性能差异的重要来源——尚未得到充分探索。本研究旨在揭示现代语音模型如何编码这一现象。

Method: 采用说话人无关的层级探测（layer-wise probing）方法，对wav2vec2-base和Whisper-small两个预训练语音模型进行两项任务测试：(1) 音段简化检测——判断是否发生CCR；(2) 音段复原——从简化形式恢复底层辅音簇的身份。

Result: 两个模型在区分简化形式与完整形式（canonical forms）上均达到高准确率。关键发现是：被简化的音段仍保留了底层塞音的语音线索，表明模型将CCR编码为结构化的梯度音系变异，而非单纯的音段删除。

Conclusion: 现代语音模型内部确实编码了AAE中CCR的结构化音系模式，这为了解语音模型的语言学表征能力以及改善ASR对非标准变体的公平性提供了重要启示。

Abstract: Self-supervised and supervised speech models are increasingly used to investigate which linguistic information their internal representations encode, and at what level of abstraction they encode it. One underexplored phenomenon is consonant cluster reduction (CCR) in African American English (AAE), a widespread phonological process and a source of automatic speech recognition (ASR) disparity. To examine how CCR is represented, we conduct speaker-independent layer-wise probing of wav2vec2-base and Whisper-small using two tasks: segmental reduction detection and segmental restoration of underlying cluster identity. Both models distinguish reduced and canonical forms with high accuracy. Crucially, reduced segments retain cues to their underlying stops, indicating that CCR is encoded as structured gradient phonological variation rather than simple segmental deletion. These results demonstrate structured phonological encoding of AAE CCR patterns in modern speech models.

</details>


### [128] [Does My Embedding Reflect That $A = B$? Evaluating Mathematical Equivalence in Embedding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23959)
*Jiaying Ye,Samarth Rao,Leo Carlin,Kedar Chintalapati,Saharsh Bhargava,Rachit Jaiswal,Michael Zhou,Jared Darlington,Jarod Alper,Vasily Ilin,Henry Kvinge*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 数学陈述因其高度抽象性，在不同子领域中表述差异巨大。本文提出MELD数据集（数学等价但词汇不同的陈述对），发现当前SOTA嵌入模型倾向于按术语而非底层数学进行分组。为此，作者提出一种对比学习方法，通过对齐非正式陈述与不同形式化表述来改进数学文本嵌入，在非正式-形式化检索和MELD上均取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学的高度抽象性使得同一数学内容在不同子领域中表述迥异，历史上许多突破源于研究者发现某个问题已在另一领域被解决。同时，形式化相关资源的增长增加了对跨数学"语言"（如Lean到自然语言）高效导航工具的需求。然而，现有嵌入模型是否能捕捉数学等价性尚不明确，这促使作者开展本研究。

Method: （1）构建MELD数据集：收集数学上等价但词汇表述差异很大的自然语言陈述对；（2）评估现有SOTA嵌入模型在MELD上的表现；（3）提出一种对比学习方法，专注于将非正式数学陈述与不同形式化表述进行对齐，以学习更好的数学文本嵌入。

Result: 当前SOTA嵌入模型倾向于根据表述所用的术语（而非底层数学内容）对陈述进行分组，在MELD上表现不佳。所提出的对比学习方法不仅在非正式-形式化检索任务上取得改进，在仅含自然语言陈述的MELD数据集上也表现出提升。

Conclusion: 现有嵌入模型未能有效捕捉数学等价性，过度依赖表面术语。通过对比学习对齐非正式与形式化数学表述，可以有效改善嵌入的数学语义表达能力，提升跨语言/跨形式的数学检索效果。

Abstract: Because mathematics is highly abstract, a single statement can take very different forms depending on what subfield it is framed in. There are many examples where breakthroughs occurred after researchers discovered that a question had already been answered in a different field. At the same time, the growth of new resources related to formalization has increased the need for tools that enable efficient and reliable navigation between mathematical 'languages' (e.g., from Lean to natural language). In this paper, we investigate whether current embedding models capture mathematical equivalence. To do this, we introduce the Mathematically Equivalent but Lexically Different Pairs (MELD) Dataset, a collection of mathematically equivalent statements that are expressed in very different language. We show that current state-of-the-art embedding models tend to group statements by the terminology used to make them instead of the underlying math. Motivated by this, we propose a contrastive approach to learning embeddings of mathematical text that focuses on aligning informal statements with different formalizations. Our experiments demonstrate that this leads to improvements not only on informal-formal retrieval tasks but also on MELD, which only contains natural language statements.

</details>


### [129] [Faithful by Construction: Claim-Anchored Attribution for Multi-Document Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23989)
*Shuo Guan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CAMS 将经典的“抽取—选择—重写”模块化摘要范式重新诠释为以声明为锚点的归因单元，构建了一个内在支持细粒度、多源可追溯的摘要框架，在保持摘要质量的同时显著提升了忠实度和引用精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端 LLM 虽能生成流畅的多文档摘要，但幻觉问题严重，且其归因通常是事后生成的粗粒度（文档/段落级）标注，难以逐句溯源验证。因此需要一种“归因优先、忠实优先”的模块化框架，从结构上保证细粒度可追溯性。

Method: 提出 CAMS（Claim-Anchored Multi-document Summarization）框架，分为四步：(1) 从每个源文档抽取原子声明并附带 token 级出处；(2) 跨文档聚类等价声明并标记源间冲突；(3) 基于支持度与显著性选择声明子集；(4) 将选中的声明重写为摘要，每句锚定一个经过支持度验证的声明，声明再反向链接到源文档的具体片段。框架内嵌支持度感知选择、约束重写与验证机制以促进（但不保证）事实忠实度。

Result: 在 MultiNews 上评估质量、忠实度与定位能力；在 DiverseSumm 上分析冲突处理；在 WCEP 上测试零样本迁移。采用双机制协议：分离参考无关的引用质量评估与基于 gold 对齐的定位精度评估，并引入与选择/验证模型解耦的支持模型进行引用精确度审计。CAMS 在摘要质量上匹敌强端到端和片段归因基线，同时忠实度与引用精度大幅提升，多源归因准确率提高约三分之二，且暴露了端到端模型隐式存在的可调节忠实度-覆盖度权衡。

Conclusion: CAMS 证明了将归因单元前置到声明层面、并将归因内嵌于管道结构的设计思路，可以在不牺牲摘要质量的前提下，系统性地缓解多文档摘要中的幻觉与溯源困难，为构建更可信的自动摘要系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: End-to-end large language models (LLMs) produce fluent multi-document summaries but remain prone to hallucination, and the attributions they offer are typically coarse (whole documents or passages) and generated post hoc, leaving each summary statement hard to verify. We revisit the modular Extract--Select--Rewrite paradigm and recast its intermediate representation as the unit of attribution. We present CAMS, a Claim-Anchored Multi-document Summarization framework that (i) extracts atomic claims with token-level provenance from every source document, (ii) clusters equivalent claims across documents while flagging inter-source conflicts, (iii) selects a support-aware and salient subset, and (iv) rewrites the selection into a summary in which every sentence is anchored to a support-checked claim that links back to one or more source spans. Because content is localized before it is realized, the pipeline is attribution-oriented by construction and faithfulness-oriented by construction: it structurally preserves fine-grained, multi-source traceability while using support-aware selection, constrained rewriting, and verification to encourage, rather than guarantee, factual faithfulness. We evaluate quality, faithfulness, and localization on MultiNews, analyze conflict handling on DiverseSumm, and test zero-shot transfer on WCEP, using a two-regime protocol that separates reference-free citation quality from gold-aligned localization accuracy, and we add an evaluator-decoupled audit that tests citation precision with a support model never used for selection or verification. CAMS matches strong end-to-end and span-attribution baselines on summary quality while substantially improving faithfulness and citation precision, lifting multi-source attribution accuracy by roughly two-thirds, and exposing a controllable faithfulness--coverage trade-off that end-to-end models leave implicit.

</details>


### [130] [RASC+: Retrieval-Constrained LLM Adjudication for Clinical Value Set Authoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23992)
*Sumit Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对临床价值集补全任务，提出一种分阶段方法：第一阶段通过词汇感知扩展和代码展示救援检索优化候选池召回率（从0.553提升至0.730），第二阶段使用约束型LLM（GPT-5）进行候选筛选，最终在全测试集上实现宏F1 0.549，在留出发布者分层上达到0.533，显著优于直接零样本生成和SAPBert交叉编码器方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床价值集补全任务中，直接零样本LLM生成效果不佳，因为临床代码系统庞大、受版本控制且难以被语言模型可靠记忆。需要一种既保持代码可审计性（所有返回代码必须来自候选池），又能实质性提升补全性能的方法。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：(1) 候选池构建阶段，使用Qwen3进行检索，结合词汇感知扩展（vocabulary-aware expansion）和代码展示救援检索（code-display rescue retrieval），最大化召回率；(2) 候选筛选阶段，使用盲法GPT-5裁决（blinded GPT-5 adjudication），在给定候选池中约束性地选择正确代码，替代原有SAPBert交叉编码器。

Result: 在RASC基准的3,744个价值集测试集上：(1) 候选池召回率从基线0.553提升至0.730，留出发布者分层为0.655；(2) SAPBert在该扩展池上仅达宏F1 0.287（全测试）和0.233（留出发布者）；(3) GPT-5裁决将宏F1提升至0.549（全测试）和0.533（留出发布者）。

Conclusion: 检索约束下的LLM裁决能够显著改善价值集补全效果，同时在安全层面保证所有返回代码均来自可审计的候选池。该方法证明了分阶段策略（召回优化检索 + 约束型LLM筛选）在此类临床术语标准化任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Clinical value sets define the standardized terminology codes used in quality measurement, phenotyping, cohort construction, and clinical decision support. The recently introduced Retrieval-Augmented Set Completion (RASC) benchmark showed that direct zero-shot large language model (LLM) generation is poorly suited to this task: clinical code systems are large, version-controlled, and not reliably memorized by language models. We study a stage-wise alternative in which candidate-pool construction is optimized for recall and a constrained LLM adjudicator is optimized for candidate selection. On the full 3,744-value-set RASC test split, Qwen3-based retrieval with vocabulary-aware expansion and code-display rescue retrieval increases candidate-pool recall from the original RASC retrieval baseline of 0.553 to 0.730; on the held-out-publisher stratum, pool recall is 0.655. The higher-recall pool alone is not sufficient: applying the original SAPBert cross-encoder to this expanded pool gives full-test macro F1 of 0.287 and held-out-publisher macro F1 of 0.233. Replacing the stage-2 selector with blinded GPT-5 adjudication over the same pool increases full-test macro F1 to 0.549 and held-out-publisher macro F1 to 0.533. These results show that retrieval-constrained LLM adjudication can substantially improve value set completion while preserving the safety constraint that all returned codes must come from an auditable candidate pool.

</details>


### [131] [Towards Spec Learning: Inference-Time Alignment from Preference Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24004)
*Dhriti Krishnan,Tejas Goyal,Jaromir Savelka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出spec learning框架，通过少量用户指令和偏好判断，自动编译出自然语言形式的规范（specification），在推理时引导LLM行为，无需更新模型参数。该方法在偏好信号密集的专业领域数据集上，常优于DPO，且生成的规范可读、可解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前引导LLM行为的两种主流方法各有缺陷：手工设计prompt费时费力、脆弱且易出错；基于偏好的微调（如DPO）虽然更严谨，但成本高昂。本文旨在提供一种低成本、高效且可解释的替代方案。

Method: 提出spec learning框架：(1) 用户提供简短指令和少量偏好判断；(2) 系统将这些偏好信号编译为自然语言形式的规范（specification）；(3) 在推理时，将这些规范作为prompt的一部分来条件化LLM的生成，整个过程无需更新模型参数。

Result: 在偏好信号密集的专业领域数据集上，基于编译规范生成的模型响应通常优于直接偏好优化（DPO）的效果。

Conclusion: spec learning是一种轻量、高效且透明的LLM行为引导方法。其生成的规范可读性强，既是偏好信号的可解释体现，也为模型行为提供了透明度，在专业领域具有实用价值。

Abstract: Steering a large language model (LLM) toward a desired behavior typically relies on an iterative process of hand-crafting a prompt based on a careful inspection of the model's responses. This is an involved, brittle, and error-prone process. Preference-based fine-tuning is a more rigorous but often prohibitively expensive solution. We propose spec learning, a framework that relies on a brief user instruction and a small set of preference judgments. These are compiled into specifications in the form of natural-language prompts for an LLM. Specifications condition LLMs at inference time, and no parameter updates to the underlying models are required. We show that the responses generated based on the compiled specifications often outperform direct preference optimization (DPO) on datasets from specialized domains whose preference signal is dense. Unlike opaque weight updates, the resulting specifications are human-readable and double as interpretable and transparent written embodiments of the preference signal that produced them.

</details>


### [132] [Towards Version-aware Operations and Transaction Memories for Multi-layer MeMo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24040)
*Peiran Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MeMo 提出在语言模型中引入显式的多层关联矩阵记忆（CMM），将记忆、检索和遗忘作为架构操作。当知识变更可表达为 MeMo 记忆关联时，可通过编辑显式记忆而非重新训练整个模型来更新可访问知识。为此设计了版本感知操作层，将 replace、obsolete、keep-history、rollback、trace 等高层操作编译为 MeMo 原语调用序列（有序事务），并引入 V-CMM 和 T-CMM 两个辅助记忆模块来管理版本转换和事务内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型在知识变更时需要重新训练，成本高昂。如果记忆是显式的架构组件，能否通过直接编辑记忆来更新知识，从而减少或避免重训练？

Method: 提出版本感知操作层，将高层知识更新操作（替换、废弃、保留历史、回滚、追踪）编译为 MeMo 原语的有序事务（如遗忘旧序列-令牌链、记忆新链、保留历史链、记录逆程序）。引入两个辅助 CMM：Version CMM（V-CMM）将版本转换映射到事务句柄；Transaction CMM（T-CMM）存储可复用的变更内容和逆程序。支持序列级直接编辑和结构化 diff 级输入两种方式。

Result: 论文给出了评估路线，涵盖更新成功率、回滚能力、可追踪性、局部性（编辑影响范围）和事务复用性五个维度，但未在摘要中报告具体实验数值。

Conclusion: 通过将知识更新形式化为对显式记忆的事务式编辑，MeMo 框架有望在不重训练的情况下实现可控、可逆、可追踪的知识变更，为语言模型的知识管理提供了一种架构级解决方案。

Abstract: MeMo proposes language models with explicit multi-layer correlation matrix memories (CMMs), where memorization, retrieval, and forgetting are architectural operations. This paper asks how such memories can reduce the need for retraining when knowledge changes. For changes expressible as MeMo memory associations, the model's accessible knowledge can be updated by editing explicit memories rather than retraining the whole model. We propose a version-aware operation layer in which high-level operations such as replace, obsolete, keep-history, rollback, and trace are compiled into MeMo-native primitive calls over sequences and tokens. The key observation is that a version-aware operation is rarely a single MeMo association. It is an ordered transaction of primitive edits, for example forgetting one sequence-token chain, memorizing another, preserving a historical chain, and recording an inverse program. The framework introduces two auxiliary CMMs: a Version CMM (V-CMM) for mapping version transitions to transaction handles, and a Transaction CMM (T-CMM) for storing reusable change contents and inverse programs. It supports both direct sequence-level edits and structured diff-level inputs, and outlines an evaluation route for update success, rollback, traceability, locality, and transaction reuse.

</details>


### [133] [Best Preprocessing Techniques for Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24055)
*Saranzaya Magsarjav,Melissa Humphries,Jonathan Tuke,Lewis Mitchell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了Twitter情感分析中预处理技术的执行顺序对分类性能的影响，发现分词影响最大、拼写纠正影响最小，并确定了最优预处理顺序：分词→文本清洗→词干提取→停用词移除，同时指出移除停用词时应保留否定词。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Twitter情感分析中预处理对降噪和提升效率至关重要，但现有研究缺乏对预处理技术执行顺序的系统性探讨，导致实践者往往依赖高成本的探索性实验来确定预处理流程。

Method: 作者选取多种预处理技术（分词、文本清洗、词干提取、停用词移除、拼写纠正等），通过排列组合形成不同执行顺序，在Twitter数据集上使用机器学习分类器进行情感分析实验，系统比较各顺序下的模型性能差异。

Result: 拼写纠正在考虑顺序因素时影响最小，而分词影响最大；词干提取和停用词移除的顺序可互换；移除停用词时保留否定词效果更优；最优预处理顺序为：分词→文本清洗→词干提取→停用词移除。

Conclusion: 该研究为实践者提供了系统化的预处理部署方案，可免去高昂的预处理探索阶段，直接采用推荐顺序即可提升模型输出质量。

Abstract: Sentiment analysis in Twitter datasets is important because it enables monitoring public opinion on products and analysis of political and social movements. One critical step is preprocessing: the automated processing of text for machine learning algorithms. Preprocessing plays a critical role in reducing noise and improving efficiency. However, little research has systematically examined the order in which preprocessing techniques are implemented. We find that, when accounting for order, spelling correction is the least impactful preprocessing technique, whereas tokenisation is the most impactful. Stemming and stop-word removal are interchangeable, and it is better to remove stop words without removing negation. The best order for applying the preprocessing techniques was tokenisation, text cleaning, stemming, and then stopword removal. Our results provide a systematic approach for practitioners to deploy preprocessing to improve model output without the costly preprocessing exploratory phase.

</details>


### [134] [Selective Capability Unlearning in End-to-End Spoken Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24063)
*Akanksha Singh,Vinod Kumar Kurmi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了SLU系统中自回归模型存在的"能力持久化"问题——即便移除了目标意图，模型仍可通过外部供给意图前缀重建完整的意图-槽位结构。为此，作者提出BSU（Binding Subspace）框架，在表示层面隔离并衰减意图条件映射的潜在方向，从而大幅降低强制前缀下的可恢复性，同时保持保留功能的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际部署中，由于政策或安全约束，SLU系统常需移除特定功能（意图及其关联的槽位生成行为）。然而，自回归模型仅抑制目标意图并不能消除"意图→槽位"的条件映射：当意图前缀被外部提供时，模型仍能重建原有的意图-槽位结构。这一结构性缺陷（能力持久化）对安全卸载功能构成严重威胁，因此需要一种能从根本上切断该条件映射的方法。

Method: 提出Binding Subspace (BSU)框架，在表示层面工作。核心思路是：识别并隔离模型表示空间中意图条件映射所依赖的特定子空间（binding subspace），然后对该子空间方向进行衰减，从而切断"意图→槽位"的条件生成通路。该方法不依赖于修改模型架构或重新训练，而是通过对表示空间的干预来实现功能移除。

Result: 在多个SLU基准测试上，BSU显著降低了强制前缀下的可恢复性（即外部给定意图前缀时模型重建原意图-槽位结构的能力大幅下降），同时很好地保持了被保留功能的原有性能。

Conclusion: 自回归SLU模型存在能力持久化这一此前未被充分认识的安全隐患，而BSU作为一种表示层面的干预框架，能够有效解决该问题，为安全、可控的功能移除提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Modern spoken language understanding (SLU) systems are increasingly deployed in real-world settings, where specific functionalities may need to be removed due to policy or safety constraints. In SLU, a functionality corresponds to an intent and its associated slot-generation behavior. However, in autoregressive models, suppressing a target intent does not eliminate the conditional mapping that generates slots conditioned on that intent. When the intent prefix is externally supplied, the model can reconstruct the original intent-slot structure. We identify this structural failure as \textbf{\emph{capability persistence}}. We propose \textit{\underline{B}inding \underline{S}ubspace (BSU)}, a representation-level framework that isolates and attenuates intent-conditioned directions underlying this mapping. Across SLU benchmarks, BSU substantially reduces forced-prefix recoverability while preserving retained performance.

</details>


### [135] [Sentence-Level Contextual Entrainment in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24077)
*Yang Liu,Chenhui Chu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM中从token级扩展到句子级的上下文诱引现象，发现提示中的句子（即使是反事实陈述）在推理时概率会显著升高；模型越大，该现象越弱；仅2%–4%的注意力头控制此现象，关闭它们可在不损害性能的前提下有效缓解上下文诱引。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文诱引（contextual entrainment）此前仅在token级别被发现，作者希望将其拓展到句子层面，系统考察其在不同规模、不同家族的LLM中的表现，并探索其内部机制与缓解方法。

Method: 在26个LLM（涵盖7个家族）和两个数据集（主观与客观任务）上，作者用句子的逐token平均对数概率替代单个token概率，检验句子级上下文诱引的存在性；通过注意力头分析，定位控制该现象的注意力头，并通过关闭这些头观察效果。

Result: 句子级上下文诱引普遍存在；模型规模增大时该现象减弱；仅2%–4%的注意力头负责控制上下文诱引，关闭它们可有效缓解该现象，且不影响模型整体性能。

Conclusion: 句子级上下文诱引是LLM中的一个稳健现象，规模越大越弱，且由极少数注意力头主导，这为理解LLM的上下文偏向机制和进行可控干预提供了新视角。

Abstract: Contextual entrainment, which is a newly discovered phenomenon in large language models (LLMs), refers to the tendency of a model to assign higher probabilities to tokens that appear in its context. In this work, we extend this phenomenon from the token level to the sentence level by examining the per-token mean log-probability of a sentence instead of the probabilities of individual tokens. We investigate sentence-level contextual entrainment across 26 LLMs from seven families and two datasets, which cover both subjective and objective tasks. We find that sentence-level contextual entrainment exists. This means that the sentences in the prompt (even if they are counterfactual statements) can significantly increase their probability during model inference time. As the model size increases, contextual entrainment gradually decreases. We also find that contextual entrainment is controlled by 2% to 4% of the attention heads. Turning off these attention heads can effectively mitigate contextual entrainment without hurting the model's performance.

</details>


### [136] [CAVEWOMAN: How Large Language Models Behave Under Linguistic Input and Output Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24083)
*Morayo Danielle Adeyemi,Ryan A. Rossi,Franck Dernoncourt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出Cavewoman评估协议，分别测量输入压缩和输出压缩对推理成本与任务准确性的影响。结果表明输出压缩可显著降低成本（最高3倍），而输入压缩反而因模型回复变长导致净成本上升（最高2.7倍），且准确性下降；同时发现即使回答正确，其表面文本也与无约束基线存在系统性偏离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前"精简表达以节省推理成本"的做法被广泛推广，但缺乏对压缩发生在哪个通道（用户提示 vs. 模型回复）的严格区分和量化评估。需要一种双通道评估协议来厘清输入压缩与输出压缩各自的实际效果。

Method: 提出Cavewoman双通道评估协议，从三个维度评分：任务准确性、每项实际成本、与无约束参考回复的文本一致性。在五个数据集上对八个模型（含API模型和开放权重模型）进行五个压缩级别的测试，两个通道在同一批样本上测量。还引入长度控制重评分、多重比较校正及互补语义度量来验证文本偏离的稳健性。

Result: 输出压缩在大多数API模型上实现1.4-2.4倍成本降低（最优达3倍），所有开放权重模型在公开定价下也受益。输入压缩则适得其反：五项基准均值净成本上升约1.15倍，最差数据集达1.8倍，更强压缩下达2.7倍，且准确性崩溃。非推理模型中约一半生成结果虽正确，但表面文本不再蕴含模型自身无约束基线生成的内容，该偏离在多项稳健性检验下依然显著。

Conclusion: "精简表达省token"的策略效果完全取决于压缩对象：压缩模型输出是有效的成本优化手段，而压缩输入提示则是严格的双输策略。此外，压缩引发的文本偏离现象揭示了模型行为深层变化，不能仅凭准确性指标判断压缩策略的优劣。

Abstract: "Talk short. Drop grammar. Save token." This caveman style is widely promoted as a way to cut inference cost, but whether it actually saves anything depends on which channel (the user's prompt or the model's response) is being compressed. We present Cavewoman, a two-channel evaluation protocol that scores every generation on task accuracy, realized per-item cost, and reference-text agreement against the model's unconstrained reference. We evaluate eight models on five datasets at five reduction levels, with both channels measured on the same items. Output compression cuts realized cost on most API models (1.4-2.4x per model, up to 3x in the best case) and on all four open-weight models under public-tier pricing.
