Relying exclusively on semantic similarity in multilingual narrative assessments may overestimate cultural preservation by neglecting culturally meaningful variations in narrative expression.

</details>


### [170] [Privacy-Preserving RAG via Multi-Agent Semantic Rewriting: Achieving Confidentiality Without Compromising Contextual Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24623)
*Yuanhe Zhao,Tianyu Zhang,Huafei Xing,Derek F. Wong,Jianbin Li,Tao Fang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多智能体框架，通过语义重写对检索增强生成（RAG）中的检索内容进行隐私脱敏，在保持语义核心的同时移除敏感标识符，显著降低定向攻击下的隐私泄露，且作为离线预处理模块不引入在线推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG通过引入外部知识增强了LLM，但在敏感场景下部署时，恶意提示可能导致检索内容中的隐私信息泄露。现有方法在隐私保护和语义保真度之间难以取得良好平衡。

Method: 提出三智能体协作框架：隐私提取智能体（识别敏感标识符）、语义分析智能体（理解语义结构）、重构智能体（基于语义核心重写无隐私内容）。三个智能体协同完成检索内容的语义重写脱敏，作为一次性离线预处理步骤异步执行。

Result: 在ChatDoctor和Wiki-PII数据集上评估6个LLM：LLaMA-3-8B的定向信息泄露从基线的144例降至仅1例；BLEU-1得分为0.122，优于SAGE方法的0.117；框架作为离线预处理模块，不增加在线推理延迟。

Conclusion: 该多智能体语义重写框架有效解决了RAG场景下的隐私-效用权衡问题，在显著抑制隐私泄露的同时保持了上下文保真度，且离线预处理设计保证了推理效率，具有实用价值。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation enhances large language models by incorporating external knowledge, but deploying it in sensitive scenarios risks privacy leakage via malicious prompts. To address this, we propose a multi-agent framework that sanitizes retrieved content through semantic rewriting. By employing three specialized agents for privacy extraction, semantic analysis, and reconstruction, our approach collaboratively removes sensitive identifiers while preserving the semantic core. We evaluate the framework on the ChatDoctor and Wiki-PII datasets across six large language models. Experimental results demonstrate a significant reduction in privacy leakage under targeted attacks. For instance, we reduced targeted information exposure in LLaMA-3-8B from 144 instances in the baseline to just 1. Furthermore, we maintain strong contextual fidelity with a BLEU-1 score of 0.122, outperforming the existing SAGE method's 0.117. Finally, the framework operates as an asynchronous preprocessing module, introducing no additional latency to online inference, as all rewriting is executed as a one-time offline preprocessing step. To promote reproducibility, the source code of this work is publicly available at https://github.com/foursoils/Privacy-Preserving-RAG.

</details>


### [171] [The Warrant Gap: Claim-Conditioned Re-scoring for Fact-Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24627)
*Arka Ujjal Dey,John Collomosse*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了SIFT（基于完整声明对提取的证据片段进行重新评分）和WSP（自动检测引用证据是否蕴含声明），解决了LLM事实核查系统中“支持”标签但证据不充分的问题，在多个基准上显著提升了准确率和证据忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的事实核查系统虽在标准基准上判决准确率高，但经常输出“支持”标签时，其引用的证据实际上不能支撑该声明。结构化分解本应是审查这些推理依据的自然方式，但僵化的提取协议会剥离声明上下文，导致分解后的片段失去完整语义。因此需要一种能保留完整声明上下文的证据评分方法。

Method: 提出两个核心组件：(1) SIFT（claim-conditioned re-scoring）：将提取的证据片段与完整声明进行条件化重新评分，而非独立评估片段；(2) WSP（Warranted Supports Proportion）：一种自动NLI检测指标，判断引用的证据是否蕴含声明。在FEVER、SciFact、5PILS和DP四个数据集上，基于四个开源骨干模型进行评估。

Result: SIFT在因朴素分解导致准确率下降最多27.6个百分点的数据单元上恢复了准确率，同时使WSP指标超越了直接提示方法。WSP与人工标注的金标准证据对比，达到AUC 0.92、精确率0.98的校准效果。

Conclusion: SIFT结合WSP有效解决了LLM事实核查中证据-声明对齐问题，既提升了分类准确率，又提供了可自动量化的证据忠实度指标，为构建更可信的事实核查系统提供了新方法。

Abstract: Fact-checking systems built on LLMs achieve high verdict accuracy on standard benchmarks, yet routinely output Supports labels whose cited evidence does not license the claim. Structured decomposition is the natural way to inspect those warrants, but rigid extraction protocols strip the full-claim context that facets need. We introduce SIFT -- claim-conditioned re-scoring of extracted evidence spans against the full claim -- paired with WSP (Warranted Supports Proportion), an automatic NLI check that the cited warrant entails the claim. We evaluate on FEVER, SciFact, 5PILS, and DP across four open-source backbones. SIFT recovers accuracy on cells where naive decomposition costs up to 27.6 points, while raising WSP above direct prompting; WSP itself calibrates against human gold evidence at AUC 0.92 and precision 0.98.

</details>


### [172] [Measuring User's Mental Models of Speech Translation in Human-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24644)
*HyoJung Han,Nishant Balepur,Jordan Boyd-Graber,Marine Carpuat*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过跨语言问答框架研究用户对语音翻译系统的心理模型，发现用户通过实践能逐步预测机器翻译错误，尤其是有源语言知识时，主要依赖表层错误线索；提供语音转录文本有助于改善心理模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管数百万人日常使用机器翻译工具，但人们对MT系统能力边界的心理认知（即心理模型）知之甚少。理解用户如何预测系统错误对于改善人机协作至关重要，但目前缺乏有效的研究框架。

Method: 提出基于跨语言问答的研究框架：用户阅读外语信息后回答问题，可选择接受MT输出或请求人工重新翻译。通过分析不同翻译质量下用户行为与准确率趋势，考察其预测系统错误的能力及心理模型的演化过程。

Result: （1）用户通过实践能形成更强的心理模型，尤其当具备一定源语言知识时；（2）用户主要依赖表层错误线索来判断翻译质量；（3）提供语音转录文本可帮助用户建立更好的心理模型。

Conclusion: 跨语言问答作为下游任务，为研究MT心理模型提供了有效框架，推进了对人-AI协作的理解。该范式的设计启示未来可进一步探索用户与MT系统的交互机制。

Abstract: Millions of people use machine translation (MT) tools daily, yet little is known about their perception of what systems can and cannot do. This paper studies users' mental models of speech translation systems through a new framework based on cross-lingual question answering, where users either accept MT output or request professional re-translation to answer questions based on the information presented in a foreign language. By analyzing user behavior and accuracy trends across varying translation qualities, we examine to what extent they can predict where the system is likely to be wrong, and how this mental model evolves. Users develop stronger mental models with practice, especially when they have some knowledge of the source language, primarily by relying on surface-level error cues. Moreover, providing speech transcriptions can help users develop better mental models. Our results show the promise of cross-lingual question answering as a downstream task for studying MT mental models and advancing our understanding of human-AI collaboration.

</details>


### [173] [Harmonic: Hierarchical State Space Models for Efficient Long-Context Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24650)
*Petr Nyoma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Harmonic 是一种用于语言建模的层次化状态空间模型，通过三层递进式慢时间尺度的循环结构，每层接收下层预测误差而非原始隐状态。在同等算力预算下，Harmonic 在长序列建模上显著优于 Transformer 和 Mamba，并能突破 RoPE 位置编码的长度限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 的自注意力计算复杂度为 O(L²)，在长序列场景内存消耗巨大；现有 SSM（如 Mamba）虽为线性复杂度但在语言建模上仍有差距。作者旨在构建一种兼具线性复杂度与强长程建模能力的架构，同时解决 RoPE 位置编码对序列长度的硬性约束。

Method: 提出 Harmonic，一种三层层次化状态空间模型。关键设计：(1) 三个循环层级以逐步减慢的时间尺度堆叠；(2) 每层接收下层预测误差（而非原始隐状态）作为输入，形成层级化预测编码机制；(3) 用 HarmonicBlock 替换 Transformer 中的注意力层，构建 Hallamonic 模型，消除 RoPE 位置编码限制。

Result: 在 enwiki8 上，Harmonic 在 1K/8K/32K token 预算下分别以 1.4%/6.7%/11.4% 的优势超越同规模 Transformer（28M 参数），并在所有长度上以 0.7-1.8% 超过 Mamba。64K token 时 Transformer 和 Mamba 在 80GB H100 上内存溢出，Harmonic 成功训练至 6.169 bpt。WikiText-103 结果复现。1B 参数规模下，Hallamonic 在 1K-8K 序列长度上保持稳定损失，而 TinyLlama 在其 2K RoPE 限制之外灾难性退化（8K 长度差距达 +9.4 bpt）。

Conclusion: 层次化预测误差驱动的 SSM 架构在长序列语言建模上同时实现了线性计算复杂度和优于 Transformer/Mamba 的性能，并能从根本上消除 RoPE 位置编码的序列长度瓶颈，展现出作为长上下文基础模型骨干的潜力。

Abstract: We present Harmonic, a hierarchical state space model (SSM) for language modeling. The architecture stacks three recurrent levels at progressively slower timescales; each level receives the prediction error of the level below as input, rather than its raw hidden state. On enwiki8 with equal token budgets, Harmonic outperforms a comparable Transformer (28M params) by +1.4% at 1K tokens, +6.7% at 8K tokens, and +11.4% at 32K tokens (bpt, lower is better). It also outperforms Mamba at every tested length by 0.7--1.8%. At 64K tokens, both Mamba and Transformer run out of memory on an 80GB H100; Harmonic trains successfully, reaching 6.169 bpt. Results replicate on WikiText-103 (H-TF gap +1.7% to +7.2% across 1K--32K). At 1B parameter scale, replacing all attention layers in TinyLlama 1.1B with HarmonicBlock eliminates the RoPE positional encoding limit: the resulting Hallamonic model maintains stable loss across sequence lengths 1K--8K on two independent clean benchmarks (Lambada and fineweb-edu held-out), while TinyLlama degrades catastrophically past its 2K-token RoPE limit (gap: +9.4 bpt at seq=8K on Lambada). Compute is O(L) per forward pass vs. O(L^2) for attention.
  Logs: https://github.com/Omibranch/harmonic-logs.

</details>


### [174] [AI-PAVE-Br: Leveraging Large Language Models for Enhanced Product Attribute Value Extraction through a Golden Set Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24655)
*Murilo Gazzola,Hugo Gobato Souto,Samuel Silva,Júlia Schubert Peixoto,Felipe Siqueira,André Luis Pedroso de Morais,Caio Gomes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AI-PAVEBr系统，利用大语言模型（LLM）结合提示工程，专门针对巴西葡萄牙语电商领域进行产品属性值抽取（PAVE），并发布了一个名为Golden Set的人工标注基准数据集。实验表明该方法显著优于传统命名实体识别（NER）基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巴西电商市场产品数据爆炸式增长且高度复杂，传统PAVE方法难以应对葡萄牙语产品的语言细微差异和描述多样性，缺乏专门针对该非英语市场的鲁棒解决方案和公开基准数据集。

Method: 1) 构建AI-PAVEBr系统，基于大语言模型（LLM）并结合定向提示工程（targeted prompt engineering）进行产品属性值抽取；2) 创建并公开Golden Set数据集——一个经人工精细标注的葡萄牙语PAVE参考集，包含实体（Entity）、类别（Category）、子类别（Subcategories）三层结构。

Result: AI-PAVEBr系统在巴西葡萄牙语产品属性值抽取任务上显著优于传统的命名实体识别（NER）基线方法，验证了LLM结合提示工程在该领域的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为巴西这一重要非英语电商市场提供了可扩展的高精度PAVE解决方案，同时通过公开发布Golden Set数据集，为NLP社区未来的PAVE研究贡献了宝贵资源。

Abstract: The explosive growth and complexity of product data within the dynamic Brazilian e-commerce landscape demand robust and specialized methods for structured information extraction. Traditional approaches to Product Attribute Value Extraction (PAVE) often struggle with the linguistic nuances and sheer diversity of product descriptions in Portuguese. To address this critical gap, this paper introduces two major contributions. First, we present AI-PAVEBr, a specialized system engineered with Large Language Models (LLMs) to perform high-accuracy PAVE specifically for Brazilian e-commerce catalogs. Second, to facilitate reproducible research and provide a definitive benchmark, we introduce and share the Golden Set, a new, meticulously curated, and manually annotated dataset for PAVE in Portuguese. We detail the creation process and structure (Entity, Category, Subcategories) of this high-quality reference set. Our experiments conclusively show that AI-PAVE-Br, leveraging targeted prompt engineering, dramatically outperforms conventional Named Entity Recognition (NER) baselines. This work not only delivers a superior, scalable solution for a major non-English market but also enriches the NLP community with a valuable, publicly available resource for future PAVE research.

</details>


### [175] [DREAM: Dense Retrieval Embeddings via Autoregressive Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24667)
*Yixuan Tang,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DREAM 利用冻结大语言模型的自回归下一词预测损失，通过将检索器生成的相似度分数注入 LLM 注意力头，为密集检索器的训练提供监督信号，在 BEIR 和 RTEB 基准上一致超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统密集检索器依赖对比学习，需要昂贵的正负文档标注对。作者探索能否利用 LLM 自回归下一词预测目标来替代人工标注，提供廉价的训练监督：若文档与查询相关，则基于该文档生成目标文本应更容易。

Method: 提出 DREAM 框架：将检索器生成的查询-文档相似度分数注入冻结 LLM 的选定注意力头中。这些分数在 LLM 预测目标输出时，决定各候选文档获得的注意力权重。由此产生的预测损失通过注意力机制反向传播梯度，对检索器进行训练。整个过程中 LLM 参数保持冻结。

Result: 在 BEIR 和 RTEB 检索基准上，使用 0.5B 至 3B 参数的嵌入骨干模型进行评测，DREAM 在不同模型规模下一致超越现有基线方法。

Conclusion: DREAM 证明通过自回归建模训练密集检索器是一条有前景的技术路线，能有效利用 LLM 的预测能力为检索器提供监督。

Abstract: Dense retrieval embedding models are a fundamental component of modern retrieval-based AI systems. Most dense retrievers are trained with contrastive objectives, which require labeled positive and negative document pairs that are often costly and difficult to obtain. In this work, we investigate whether the autoregressive next-token prediction objective of a large language model (LLM) can provide supervision for dense retrieval. The intuition is simple: if a document contains information relevant to a query, conditioning on that document should make the target output easier for the LLM to predict. A key challenge is that the next-token prediction loss is computed inside the LLM, while the retriever is a separate embedding model. To address this challenge, we propose DREAM (Dense Retrieval Embeddings via Autoregressive Modeling), which injects retriever-generated query-document similarity scores into selected attention heads of a frozen LLM. During training, these scores determine how much attention each candidate document receives while the LLM predicts the target output. The resulting prediction loss provides gradients for retriever training through the attention mechanism. We evaluate DREAM on retrieval benchmarks BEIR and RTEB using embedding backbones ranging from 0.5B to 3B parameters. DREAM consistently outperforms existing baselines across different model scales. These results demonstrate that DREAM provides a promising approach for training dense retrievers through autoregressive modeling.

</details>


### [176] [CN-NewsTTS Bench: a target-level automatic benchmark for raw-input Chinese news TTS pronunciation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24714)
*Shijun Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了 CN-NewsTTS Bench v0.1，一个用于评估中文新闻 TTS 系统对密集书写形式（如分数、型号、范围、单位符号等）发音正确性的开放基准，包含数据集、自动评分器及 7 款产品 TTS 的初始评测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中文新闻文本包含大量密集书写形式（分数、带连字符的型号名、范围、单位符号、百分比、英文缩写、中拉数字混合名称等），这些形式在实际听感流程中频繁出现，而 TTS 系统可能在保留书写字符串的同时改变其口语含义。现有系统往往依赖用户侧规则、LLM 重写、SSML 提示或人工编辑来解决这些问题，缺乏一个无需这些辅助手段的、直接评估从原始文本出发发音正确性的基准。

Method: 构建 CN-NewsTTS Bench v0.1 基准：包含 200 条开发集、800 条公开测试集、992 个可自动评估的目标项；使用三个 ASR 系统的集成结果固定转录文本；开发自动目标评分器；对 7 款产品级 TTS 系统进行评测，并报告 ASR 路由诊断、ASR 子集消融、类别级结果、置信区间及提供商配置元数据等分析。

Result: 最佳系统的严格准确率达到 0.879，而多个系统的准确率低于 0.60。评分器能够自动评估目标发音正确性，无需人工标注。

Conclusion: 中文新闻 TTS 在处理密集书写形式的发音正确性方面仍存在显著差距，CN-NewsTTS Bench 为这一领域提供了标准化的自动评估框架，揭示了当前产品系统的能力上限和改进空间。

Abstract: Chinese news text contains dense written forms such as scores, hyphenated model names, ranges, unit symbols, percentages, English abbreviations, and mixed Chinese-Latin-digit names. These forms are frequent in real listening workflows, and a text-to-speech (TTS) system can preserve the written string while changing the spoken meaning. We introduce CN-NewsTTS Bench v0.1, an open target-level benchmark for evaluating whether Chinese news TTS products pronounce such targets correctly from raw text, without user-side rules, LLM rewriting, SSML hints, or manual edits. The release contains a 200-record development set, an 800-record public test set, 992 public auto-evaluable targets, fixed transcripts from a three-ASR ensemble, an automatic target scorer, and initial results for seven product TTS systems. We additionally report ASR-route diagnostics, ASR-subset ablations, category-level results, confidence intervals, and provider configuration metadata. The best system reaches 0.879 strict accuracy, while several systems remain below 0.60.

</details>


### [177] [Task Decomposition for Efficient Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24734)
*Nupoor Gandhi,Emma Strubell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将复杂结构化标注任务分解为子任务，以降低标注项目的整体推理负荷。作者借鉴向心理论中的"中心"概念，建立了基于有效标注空间自由度的推理负荷形式化模型，并提供了任务分解指南及在固定预算下分配子任务以最大化质量的流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量结构化标注成本高昂：人工标注费时费力，模型标注虽成本低但需昂贵验证。传统工作流中单个标注者端到端完成整个示例，但结构化标注复杂，不同方面对标注者构成不同的推理负荷。现代标注项目可融合异构标注者（模型与不同专长的人类），但如何针对不同标注挑战在异构标注者间差异化分配工作仍不明确。

Method: 1) 借鉴向心理论（Centering Theory）中的"中心"概念，提出将标注任务分解为子任务；2) 建立推理负荷的形式化模型，基于有效标注空间中的自由度来量化；3) 证明识别"中心"（即标注子任务所实现的显著锚实体）可约束输出空间复杂度，且隔离并推进中心识别的分解能降低总体推理负荷；4) 提供分解复杂结构化标注任务的指南；5) 提出在固定预算下跨标注者分配子任务以最大化质量的流程。

Result: 通过形式化模型证明：识别中心实体可约束输出空间复杂度；将中心识别隔离为独立子任务的分解策略能有效降低整体推理负荷。以作者先前工作中的实例展示了该方法带来的成本效益改进。

Conclusion: 将复杂结构化标注任务分解为中心识别驱动的子任务，可系统性地降低推理负荷。通过形式化模型和任务分配流程，能够在固定预算约束下实现标注质量最大化，为异构标注者环境下的标注项目设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: High-quality annotations of structured representations are expensive to collect over large corpora. Manual annotation of structure is laborious, and model-based annotation, although cheaper to generate, requires expensive validation and potentially significant supervision to ensure that the annotation quality is strong enough to be useful downstream. In traditional annotation workflows, annotation of each complete example is performed end-to-end by a single annotator. However, structured annotation is complex, and each aspect of the task represents a unique challenge with an associated inferential load for a given annotator. Modern annotation projects can incorporate heterogeneous groups of annotators, including both models and human annotators with varying domain and linguistic expertise. It remains unclear, however, how to redesign annotation tasks in this setting, where efforts are discriminately allocated across heterogeneous annotators with respect to distinct annotation challenges.
  We propose to decompose annotation tasks into sub-tasks in order to reduce the aggregate inferential load of annotation projects. Inspired by the notion of centers from centering theory, we introduce a formal model of inferential load based on the degrees of freedom in the space of valid annotations. Using this model, we show that identifying these centers (i.e. salient anchor entities realized by annotation sub-tasks) constrains the output space complexity, and decompositions which isolate and advance center identification reduce the aggregate inferential load. We provide guidelines for decomposing complex structured annotation tasks, supported by examples demonstrating improved cost-efficiency from our prior work. Finally, we present a procedure for allocating sub-tasks across annotators to maximize quality under a fixed budget.

</details>


### [178] [CANDLE: Character-level Arabic Noise Deduplication using Lightweight Encoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24758)
*Faris Alasmary,Taif Nono,Orjuwan Zaafarani,Kholood Al Tabash,Ahmad Ghannam,Anas Salamah,Shouq Sadah,Lahouari Ghouti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CANDLE是一个轻量级的阿拉伯语字符级噪声去重系统，将去重任务建模为CTC序列对齐问题，无需手工规则或词典。在三个基准上SER低至5.37%，通过知识蒸馏将6层模型压缩为2层，实现3倍深度缩减。去重后，阿拉伯语LLM分词器的fertility降低高达12.8%，直接降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体文本中普遍存在字符重复现象（如"helloooo"），既可能是正确拼写，也可能是非正式的字符拉长。阿拉伯语因丰富的形态学和方言变体，这一问题尤为突出。传统方法依赖手工规则、词典或形态分析器，难以泛化且维护成本高。因此需要一种轻量、可学习、无需语言特异性资源的去重方案。

Method: 核心创新是将CTC（Connectionist Temporal Classification）应用于字符去重，将其建模为序列对齐问题：基于字符级编码器，CTC学习对齐原始噪声文本与规范化文本之间的映射，自然处理可变长度的重复序列。具体做法：6层Transformer编码器上进行CTC训练；为进一步降低推理开销，采用知识蒸馏将6层教师模型压缩为2层学生模型，实现3倍深度缩减。

Result: 在三个基准数据集（干净报纸文本、人工标注歧义案例、真实社交媒体文本）上评估：CTC模型SER低至5.37%，大幅优于分类基线；蒸馏后的2层模型性能下降极小；下游评估显示，去重使多种阿拉伯语LLM分词器的fertility相对降低最高12.8%，有效减少token消耗、改善上下文窗口利用率。

Conclusion: CANDLE证明CTC可以有效地用于字符级去重任务，作为一种无需手工规则和语言特异性资源的轻量方案，在阿拉伯语上取得了优异效果，且能通过知识蒸馏大幅压缩模型。去重还对下游LLM分词效率产生显著正向影响，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Handling repeated characters in text can be tricky, since they can represent either the correct spelling of a word or informal character elongation often seen in social media posts. We present CANDLE, a lightweight system for character-level Arabic noise deduplication that addresses this challenge without relying on handcrafted rules, dictionaries, or morphological analyzers. At the heart of CANDLE is a novel application of Connectionist Temporal Classification (CTC) to this task, a formulation not previously explored for character deduplication, which frames normalization as a sequence alignment problem over a character-based encoder. Evaluated on three benchmarks spanning clean newspaper, manually curated ambiguous cases, and real-world social media text, the CTC model achieves a Sentence Error Rate (SER) as low as $5.37\%$ and consistently outperforms a classification-based baseline by a large margin. To reduce inference overhead, we distill the 6-layer CTC model into a 2-layer student, achieving a $3\times$ depth reduction with minimal performance degradation. Beyond deduplication accuracy, normalization yields a practical downstream benefit: a relative reduction in tokenizer fertility of up to $12.8\%$ across a diverse set of Arabic LLM tokenizers, directly lowering inference costs and improving context window utilization. We release all code and models publicly to support reproducibility and advance future research\footnote{https://github.com/abjadai/candle}.

</details>


### [179] [Posterior Refinement: Fast Language Generation via Any-Order Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24773)
*Manan Agarwal,Sheel Shah,Chanhyuk Lee,Jaehoon Yoo,Jerry Huang,Seunghoon Hong,Aditi Raghunathan,Jinwoo Kim,Nicholas M. Boffi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FMLM+ 在 Flow Map Language Model (FMLM) 中引入掩码式噪声调度，并提出 Posterior Refinement 推理时自修正策略，在保持 FMLM 少步生成优势的同时，弥补了其推理灵活性的不足，以极少的 NFE 实现了与离散基线相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非自回归生成模型存在两难困境：Masked Diffusion Models (MDMs) 支持灵活的迭代修正，但因分解误差导致多 token 同时生成时质量崩塌；FMLM 通过联合序列传输实现出色的少步生成，却牺牲了 MDM 的推理时灵活性。本文旨在弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 提出 FMLM+ 框架，为 FMLM 配备掩码式噪声调度，使模型在单步生成完整序列的同时，能够后验地评估每个 token 的全局一致性。基于此引入 Posterior Refinement 策略，在推理时自适应地自我修正输出。

Result: Posterior Refinement 以比离散基线少 32 倍 NFE 的计算量达到了相当的性能。在多个基准上，FMLM+ 结合 Posterior Refinement 在速度-质量权衡上超越了 MDM 和 FMLM 系列模型。

Conclusion: FMLM+ 成功统一了 FMLM 的高效少步生成与 MDM 的灵活迭代修正能力，为高保真语言建模提供了可扩展的基础框架。

Abstract: Non-autoregressive generation offers a powerful paradigm for iterative refinement, allowing models to recursively critique, erase and regenerate arbitrary subsets of tokens. However, existing non-autoregressive models fail to realize this potential. Masked Diffusion Models (MDMs) suffer from factorization error, causing sample quality to collapse when generating multiple tokens simultaneously. Flow Map Language Models (FMLMs) circumvent this bottleneck via joint sequence transport for excellent few-step generation, but sacrifice the inference-time flexibility of MDMs. We introduce FMLM+, a framework that bridges this gap by equipping FMLM with masking-style noise schedules. While generating the full sequence in a single step, FMLM+ simultaneously scores the global consistency of each token a posteriori. We leverage this to introduce Posterior Refinement, a novel inference-time refinement strategy that enables the model to adaptively self-correct its outputs, matching the performance of discrete baselines with 32x fewer NFEs. Across diverse benchmarks, we demonstrate that FMLM+ with Posterior Refinement improves the speed--quality tradeoff over both MDM and FMLM families, providing a scalable foundation for high-fidelity language modeling.

</details>


### [180] [Are We Ready For An Agent-Native Memory System?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24775)
*Wei Zhou,Xuanhe Zhou,Shaokun Han,Hongming Xu,Guoliang Li,Zhiyu Li,Feiyu Xiong,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从数据管理视角系统性地研究了LLM智能体的记忆系统，将记忆架构分解为表示与存储、提取、检索与路由、维护四个核心模块，在11个数据集上评估了12种记忆系统，揭示了不同模块的消融影响、成本-性能权衡，以及"局部维护优于全局重组"等关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆评测主要采用端到端任务成功指标（如F1、BLEU），将记忆系统视为黑盒，忽视了操作成本、模块间架构权衡以及动态知识更新下的鲁棒性等系统级问题。亟需从数据管理视角进行细粒度的系统性分析。

Method: 提出将智能体记忆解构为四个核心模块的分析框架：记忆表示与存储、记忆提取、检索与路由、记忆维护。在此框架下，选取12种代表性记忆系统和2个参考基线，在覆盖11个数据集的5类基准工作负载上进行端到端评估，并通过细粒度消融实验量化各模块对表示保真度、检索精度、更新正确性和长期稳定性的独立影响。

Result: 端到端评估显示没有任何单一架构能在所有场景中占优，效果取决于记忆结构与工作负载瓶颈的匹配程度。消融实验量化了各模块对表示保真度、检索精度、更新正确性和长期稳定性的贡献差异。成本-性能分析揭示局部维护比全局重组更具成本效率。

Conclusion: 构建真正智能体原生的记忆系统需要根据工作负载瓶颈定制记忆结构，采用局部维护策略以平衡成本与性能，并关注模块间的协同设计。本文为未来研究指明了若干有前景的方向。

Abstract: Memory for large language model (LLM) agents has rapidly evolved from simple retrieval-augmented mechanisms into a data management system that supports persistent information storage, retrieval, update, consolidation, and dynamic lifecycle governance throughout agent execution. Despite this evolution, existing evaluations still benchmark agent memory mainly through end-to-end task success metrics (e.g., F1, BLEU), while treating the underlying system as a monolithic black box. As a result, critical system-level concerns, including operational costs, architectural trade-offs across memory modules, and robustness under dynamic knowledge updates, remain insufficiently explored. In this paper, we present a systematic experimental study of agent memory from a data management perspective. We propose an analytical framework that decomposes agent memory into four core modules: memory representation and storage, extraction, retrieval and routing, and maintenance. Under this framework, we evaluate 12 representative memory systems and two reference baselines across five benchmark workloads spanning 11 datasets. Our extensive end-to-end evaluation shows that no single architecture dominates across all scenarios; instead, effectiveness depends heavily on how well the memory structure aligns with the workload bottleneck. Furthermore, through fine-grained ablation studies, we quantify their individual effects on representation fidelity, retrieval precision, update correctness, and long-horizon stability. Finally, we reveal cost-performance trade-offs under realistic workloads, showing localized maintenance is more cost-efficient than global reorganization. Based on these findings, we identify promising directions towards building truly agent-native memory systems. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/OpenDataBox/MemoryData.

</details>


### [181] [Paying to Know: Micro-Transaction Markets for Verified Product Information in Agentic E-Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24783)
*Filippos Ventirozos,Matthew Shardlow*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出，随着自主购物代理和微支付基础设施的成熟，电商的核心瓶颈已从"商品匹配"转向"可信决策信息的获取"。作者构想了一个基于微交易的验证信息市场，买方代理按需付费解锁卖家与评论者提供的各类可信数据，并由此梳理出若干新的NLP研究问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前商业NLP领域将购物聊天机器人定位为推荐系统或转化工具，其核心任务是匹配用户与商品目录并促成交易。然而，当买方变成能够自主穷举调查的智能代理（agent）时，稀缺资源不再是商品匹配能力，而是关于商品的可靠、决策相关信息。这一根本性转变要求重新定义电商NLP的研究议程。

Method: 论文采用愿景驱动（vision-driven）的论证方法：首先分析agent-native微支付通道（如x402、AP2）带来的经济稀缺性转移；其次勾勒一个"验证信息微交易市场"的架构——买方代理以不足一美分的代价逐步解锁服务履历、第三方检测报告、物料清单、经审计的销售与支持指标等卖方与评论者提供的数据，采用freemium模式按需付费，评论者信任度通过声誉评分机制量化；最后，将该愿景转化为具体的NLP技术问题。

Result: 论文提出，该架构奖励真实产品品质，并能产生比基于排名的店面更真实的竞争。同时，识别出五项核心NLP问题：成本最优的信息获取、数据定价与谈判、实时实体解析、有锚定的价值交换，以及隐私保护的画像建模，并主张这些方向而非对话流畅性应成为该领域的研究重点。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁NLP研究社区将注意力从聊天机器人的对话流畅性转向上述信息经济学驱动的核心问题，从而为"代理式电商"（agentic e-commerce）构建技术基础。

Abstract: Commercial NLP treats the shopping chatbot as a recommender or a conversion tool: its job is to match a user to a catalogue entry and close a sale. We argue that the arrival of agent-native micro-payment rails (e.g., x402, AP2) changes what is scarce. When the buyer is an autonomous agent that can investigate exhaustively, the bottleneck is no longer matching products but acquiring trustworthy, decision-relevant information about them. We envision agentic e-commerce as a micro-transaction market for verified information: buyer agents spend fractions of a cent to progressively unlock seller- and reviewer-supplied data -- service histories, third-party test reports, bills of materials, audited sales and support metrics -- paid for a la carte under a freemium model, with reviewer trust scored reputationally. We sketch the architecture of such a market and argue that it rewards genuine product quality and yields truer competition than ranking-based storefronts. We then translate the vision into concrete NLP problems -- cost-optimal information acquisition, data pricing and negotiation, real-time entity resolution, grounded value exchange, and privacy-preserving persona modelling -- and argue that these, not chat fluency, deserve the field's attention.

</details>


### [182] [SHERLOC: Structured Diagnostic Localization for Code Repair Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24820)
*Hovhannes Tamoyan,Sean Narenthiran,Erik Arakelyan,Mira Mezini,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SHERLOC 是一个无需训练的故障定位框架，利用推理型LLM结合轻量仓库工具和自我修复机制，在SWE-Bench上达到SOTA定位精度，并能显著提升修复智能体的解决率，同时降低token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体在解决仓库级编码任务时，约一半的token预算消耗在故障定位上。现有的定位框架仅被评估为文件检索任务，而非可操作的诊断，缺乏修复智能体所需的诊断上下文。

Method: SHERLOC采用结构化假设驱动的探索与推理框架，将推理型LLM与紧凑的仓库工具和自我修复（self-recovery）机制配对，无需微调或多智能体编排。

Result: 在SWE-Bench Lite上达到84.33% accuracy@1，在SWE-Bench Verified上达到81.27% recall@1；约30B参数规模即可匹敌或超越其他智能体方法。将SHERLOC的定位和诊断结果注入修复智能体后，SWE-Bench Verified上的解决率平均提升5.95个百分点，定位和总token消耗分别降低36.7%和23.1%。

Conclusion: SHERLOC证明了无需训练的假设驱动定位框架可以有效替代当前智能体中低效的定位过程，既提升定位精度又为下游修复提供有价值的诊断上下文，从而整体提升仓库级代码修复的效率与效果。

Abstract: LLM agents solve repository-level coding tasks through multi-turn tool use, but utilize half their budget on locating faults before editing. Dedicated localization frameworks have emerged, yet are still evaluated as file retrieval rather than actionable diagnosis, producing locations without the diagnostic context a repair agent needs. We introduce SHERLOC (Structured Hypothesis-driven Exploration and Reasoning for Localization), a training-free framework pairing a reasoning LLM with compact repository tools and self-recovery, without fine-tuning or multi-agent orchestration. SHERLOC reaches state-of-the-art localization across model scales: 84.33% accuracy@1 on SWE-Bench Lite and 81.27% recall@1 on SWE-Bench Verified; at ~30B parameters, it matches or outperforms other agentic methods. Injecting our locations and diagnostic findings into repair agents yields, on average, +5.95 pp resolve rate on SWE-Bench Verified while cutting localization and total tokens by 36.7% and 23.1%.

</details>


### [183] [L3Cube-MahaPOS: A Marathi Part-of-Speech Tagging Dataset and BERT Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24825)
*Hariom Ingle,Ronit Ghode,Ishwari Gondkar,Jidnyasa Harad,Raviraj Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了L3Cube-MahaPOS，一个包含32,354句人工标注的马拉地语词性标注数据集，遵循16标签的Universal Dependencies方案，并在六种模型族上进行了基准测试，最佳系统达到88.67%的词级准确率和81.67%的宏F1值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 马拉地语虽拥有超过8300万使用者，但在标注语料和标准化评估基准方面严重缺乏资源。其丰富的形态变化、相对自由的语序、缺乏大小写规范以及与印地语和英语的广泛语码混合，使得该语言的词性标注极具挑战性，迫切需要高质量的标注数据集来推动相关研究。

Method: 构建了L3Cube-MahaPOS数据集，包含32,354句新闻文本，由熟练掌握马拉地语的标注团队按照16标签的Universal Dependencies方案进行全手工标注。设计了一套涵盖Unicode规范化、天城体感知分词和噪声过滤的结构化预处理流水线以确保标签一致性。在该数据集上对HMM、CRF、BiLSTM、BiLSTM+CharCNN、MuRIL和MahaBERT-v2六类模型进行了基准评测。

Result: 最佳系统在15个评估标签类别上取得了88.67%的词级准确率和81.67%的宏F1值。

Conclusion: L3Cube-MahaPOS为马拉地语NLP提供了一个高质量的基准数据集，填补了该语言在词性标注资源上的空白。数据集、标注指南及模型检查点均已开源，有望推动马拉地语NLP的后续研究。

Abstract: Part-of-Speech (POS) tagging is a foundational NLP task underpinning machine translation, information extraction, and syntactic parsing. Despite Marathi being spoken by over 83 million people and ranking among the top twenty most spoken languages worldwide, it remains severely under-resourced in annotated corpora and standardised evaluation benchmarks. Marathi presents unique challenges for computational modelling owing to its rich morphology, relatively free word order, lack of capitalisation conventions, and pervasive code-mixing with Hindi and English. We introduce L3Cube-MahaPOS, a gold-standard POS tagging dataset for Marathi comprising 32,354 manually annotated sentences drawn from news text. Annotation was performed entirely manually by a team of Marathi-proficient annotators following a 16-tag Universal Dependencies-aligned scheme. A structured preprocessing pipeline covering Unicode normalisation, Devanagari-aware tokenisation, and noise filtering ensures label consistency across all splits. We benchmark the dataset across six model families spanning HMM, CRF, BiLSTM, BiLSTM+CharCNN, MuRIL, and the Marathi-specific transformer MahaBERT-v2. The best system achieves 88.67\% token-level accuracy and a macro-F1 of 81.67% over 15 evaluated tag classes. We release the dataset, annotation guidelines, and trained model checkpoints to foster further research in Marathi NLP.

</details>


### [184] [Less is More: Quality-Aware Training Data Selection for Scientific Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24828)
*Maria Nefeli Paraskevopoulou,Tatiana Passali,Grigorios Tsoumakas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个包含188万篇PMC文章的生物医学大规模长文档摘要数据集，并通过源对齐度和基于模型的指标分析了作者摘要的参考质量，发现基于质量信号筛选高质量子集训练可提升事实性指标并改善训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前科学长文档摘要数据集存在两大局限：一是作为金标准参考的作者摘要质量参差不齐，与原文对齐度不一；二是公开科学摘要数据集在规模和结构上不足以支撑现代长上下文模型的训练需求。

Method: (a) 构建并发布了包含188万篇PMC文章的生物医学与生命科学领域大规模长文档摘要数据集；(b) 采用源对齐度（source-grounded）指标和基于模型的（model-based）指标，系统分析作者撰写摘要的参考质量，并以此指导训练数据筛选。

Result: 作者摘要与全文的对齐度存在显著差异；利用质量信号筛选高质量子集进行训练，在匹配训练规模下优于随机采样，并在事实性导向的指标上可匹敌甚至超越更大规模的随机子集。

Conclusion: 参考质量是科学摘要中的一个重要因素，质量感知的数据选择策略能够有效提升训练效率。作者建议未来科学摘要研究应关注参考摘要的质量问题。

Abstract: Scientific long-document summarization datasets commonly treat author-written abstracts as gold reference summaries, although their quality and alignment with the source article vary. At the same time, publicly available scientific summarization datasets remain limited in scale and structure for modern long-context models. In this work, we address both challenges by a) constructing and releasing one of the largest biomedical and life science datasets for long-document summarization, containing 1.88 million PMC articles, and b) analyzing the reference quality of author-written abstracts with source-grounded and model-based metrics. We show that author-written abstracts vary in their alignment with the full article and that these quality signals can guide training-data selection. Training on selected high-quality subsets outperforms random sampling at matched training sizes and can match or exceed larger random subsets on factuality-oriented metrics. Our findings suggest that reference quality is an important factor in scientific summarization and that quality-aware data selection can improve training efficiency.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [Bias-Aware Confidence Intervals for Synthetic Control via Placebo-in-Time Bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23857)
*Song Wei,Sonia Park,Niteesh Kalangi,Jason Huang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 合成控制法的标准高斯置信区间在低强度处理下存在系统性偏差，导致错误的方向性推断。本文提出一种"时间安慰剂自助法"（placebo-in-time bootstrap），通过回推处理时间、在安慰剂时间点重新拟合模型，直接估计偏差分布并校准临界值，从而实现轨迹无关的覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数被处理群体呈现"底部厚重"的强度分布特征，此时合成控制模型的系统性偏差与处理信号相当甚至超过信号。该偏差在不同单元间同号，不会平均化，导致标准高斯置信区间收缩并收敛到错误中心——这并非精度不足，而是方向性错误。现有方法均未能有效度量该偏差。

Method: 提出"时间安慰剂自助法"（placebo-in-time bootstrap）：对每个处理单元，将处理起始时间回溯至真实处理前的多个安慰剂时间点，在每个安慰剂时间点重新拟合合成控制模型，所得"安慰剂缺口"（placebo gaps）与污染真实估计的偏差来自同一分布；通过对这些缺口进行自助抽样，获得在零假设下校准的临界值。该方法重抽样的是已实现的模型误差而非假设的处理效应。

Result: 所提方法在零假设下提供校准良好的临界值，覆盖具有固定宽度，且与真实效应的时间演化轨迹无关（trajectory-agnostic），无论效应如何随时间变化均能保持有效覆盖。

Conclusion: 针对合成控制法中普遍存在但被忽视的系统性偏差问题，时间安慰剂自助法提供了一种直接估计偏差分布、校准推断的实用方案，克服了标准高斯置信区间在低强度处理场景下的方向性失效，具有重要的方法论和实践价值。

Abstract: Synthetic control (SC) methods are among the most widely used tools for causal inference without randomization. The standard Gaussian confidence interval around the estimated effect is simple, fast, and reliably directional when the treatment signal is strong, so practitioners default to it for good reason. Most treated populations, however, are bottom-heavy in intensity, and for them the SC model's systematic bias rivals or exceeds the signal even under good pre-treatment fit. Because this bias shares sign across units it does not average out, and the Gaussian confidence interval shrinks past it and converges on a wrong center. The failure is not imprecision but misdirection: a positive effect estimated as negligible is a missed opportunity, while a negligible effect estimated as significantly positive leads to continued investment in an intervention that is not working. No existing confidence interval for the SC effect measures this bias. We propose a placebo-in-time bootstrap that estimates the bias distribution directly from the observed panel. For each treated unit the procedure backdates the treatment onset and refits the SC model at each placebo onset; the resulting placebo gaps are draws from the same bias distribution that contaminates the real estimate, and bootstrapping them yields a critical value calibrated at the zero null. Because the method resamples realized model error rather than a hypothesized effect, coverage is trajectory-agnostic: it holds at fixed width regardless of how the true effect evolves over time.

</details>


### [186] [Empirical prior distributions for treatment-by-subgroup interaction heterogeneity in random-effects meta-analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23968)
*Renato Panaro,Christian Röver,Tim Friede*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文从Cochrane系统评价数据库中超过3000项交互作用Meta分析中，推导出针对总体效应和交互效应异质性（τ）的经验先验分布。结果表明，交互效应的异质性通常远小于治疗效应的异质性，且由于交互效应估计精度较低，异质性更难识别，因此在稀疏的交互Meta分析中使用经验先验尤为有价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚组分析在获益-风险评估中至关重要，其有效结论依赖于（试验内）交互作用估计，并需考虑交互效应的异质性。当仅有少量研究时，若能利用关于异质性预期量的先验信息将有利于推断。然而，目前仅有针对总体治疗效应异质性的经验校准，缺乏针对治疗-亚组交互效应异质性的对应校准。

Method: 从Cochrane系统评价数据库（CDSR）中提取超过3000项交互Meta分析，通过大规模实证研究推导出总体效应异质性和交互效应异质性的经验（预测性）先验分布，并按效应量指标分层。进一步分析交互效应估计精度对异质性识别的影响，并通过一个激励性实例展示定制化交互效应先验在Meta分析中的应用。

Result: 1. 交互效应异质性（τ）的幅度普遍远小于治疗效应异质性；2. 试验内交互效应估计精度较低，使得交互异质性更难被识别；3. 在稀疏的交互Meta分析中，使用经验先验可显著改善估计精度。

Conclusion: 针对交互效应量身定制的经验先验能大幅提升Meta分析的精度，相比标准异质性先验具有明显优势，尤其适用于研究数量较少的稀疏交互Meta分析场景。

Abstract: Subgroup analyses are central to the assessment of benefits and risks, where recommendations may depend on evidence that treatment effects differ across patient groups. Valid subgroup claims require evidence based on (within-trial) interaction estimates while accounting for the heterogeneity in those interaction effects. In the common case of only a few available studies, inference may benefit from the use of prior information on the expected amount of heterogeneity. Although between-study heterogeneity~($τ$) has been studied empirically for overall treatment effects, no such calibration exists for treatment-by-subgroup interaction effects. We derive empirical (predictive) prior distributions for overall and interaction effect heterogeneity from over 3{,}000 interaction meta-analyses drawn from the \emph{Cochrane Database of Systematic Reviews (CDSR)}. The resulting effect-measure-specific priors indicate that interaction heterogeneity tends to be substantially smaller than treatment effect heterogeneity. We also show that lower precision of within-trial interaction estimates makes interaction heterogeneity harder to identify. Therefore, the use of empirical priors is particularly valuable in sparse interaction meta-analyses. A motivating example illustrates how priors tailored to interaction effects may substantially improve precision in a meta-analysis compared with standard heterogeneity priors.

</details>


### [187] [Inference and local influence diagnostics for unit-Lindley additive partially linear models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23972)
*Hatice T. K. Akdur,Danilo V. Silva,Gilberto A. Paula*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类单元-Lindley加性部分线性模型(UL-APLMs)，将单参数单元-Lindley分布的简洁性与加性部分线性结构的灵活性相结合，用于对取值限于单位区间的响应变量建模。模型采用B样条基结合惩罚似然框架进行估计，并通过残差分析和局部影响分析评估拟合优度与稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 响应变量受限于单位区间(0,1)的情形在心理学、医学等领域普遍存在，现有的回归方法在模型简洁性、可解释性与灵活性的平衡上存在不足。需要一种既能保持参数简约、又能同时容纳线性和非线性协变量效应的建模框架。

Method: 1) 以单参数单元-Lindley分布为响应分布构建加性部分线性模型；2) 采用B样条基函数对加性项进行建模，在惩罚似然框架下通过最大化惩罚对数似然函数进行参数估计；3) 利用残差分析评估拟合优度；4) 基于局部影响分析法，引入案例权重和响应扰动方案下的曲率诊断，评估参数估计的稳健性并识别强影响点；5) 显式推导了敏感度矩阵和惩罚观测信息矩阵。

Result: 模拟研究在多种场景下验证了估计程序的准确性；通过对垂体功能减退患者心理评估的真实数据应用，展示了模型的实用性和诊断价值。

Conclusion: UL-APLMs为受限单位区间数据提供了一种兼具简约性、灵活性与可诊断性的回归建模方案，在理论和应用层面均展现出良好性能，尤其适用于需要同时捕捉线性和非线性效应的比例/比率型数据分析场景。

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel regression framework for modeling response variables restricted to the unit interval by proposing unit-Lindley additive partially linear models (UL-APLMs). This model class combines parsimony and interpretability of one-parameter unit-Lindley distribution with the flexibility of additive partial linear structures, enabling the coexistence of linear and smooth covariate effects. Additive terms are modeled using B-spline basis under a penalized likelihood framework to ensure smoothness. Estimation is carried out by maximizing the penalized log-likelihood function. The goodness-of-fit of the models is assessed through residual analysis, whereas the robustness of the parameter estimates and the detection of influential data points are evaluated using the local influence approach, which incorporates curvature diagnostics under case-weight and response perturbation schemes. The sensitivity and penalized observed information matrices are derived explicitly for the proposed model. Simulation studies demonstrate the accuracy of the estimation procedure under various scenarios. Real data on the assessment of the psychological profile of patients with hypopituitarism illustrate the applicability of the model, highlighting the diagnostic importance.

</details>


### [188] [Inferential applications of the moments of the logit-normal distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23998)
*John Holmes,Ness Arps,Marco Reale*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对logit-normal分布矩难以解析计算的问题，本文提出一种基于logistic函数近似的矩估计方法，该方法在高达8阶矩内保持高精度，避免了Mordell积分近似的数值不稳定性，且比R中的数值积分更快。该方法适用于加速逻辑回归的期望传播算法，但不足以直接计算逻辑混合模型中的logit-normal积分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: logit-normal分布在许多推断问题中隐式出现，但缺乏默认的矩计算方法，其矩通常被认为在解析上不可得。现有方法（如Mordell积分近似）存在数值不稳定性，数值积分则计算缓慢，因此亟需一种准确、稳定且高效的logit-normal矩估计方法。

Method: 通过近似logistic函数来估计任意正整数阶的logit-normal矩。该方法基于对logistic函数的解析逼近，将logit-normal矩转化为可高效计算的闭型表达式。

Result: 该方法在高达第8阶矩上均表现出高精度；避免了Mordell积分近似在第一阶矩上的数值不稳定性；计算速度优于R中的数值积分方法。在应用方面，该近似方法能够加速逻辑回归中期望传播（EP）的实现，但对逻辑混合模型中出现的logit-normal积分直接求解仍不够通用。

Conclusion: 所提出的logit-normal矩近似方法在精度、稳定性和速度上均优于现有方法，适用于加速期望传播等特定推断任务，但在处理更一般的logit-normal积分（如逻辑混合模型）时存在局限性。

Abstract: Despite the implicit appearance of logit-normal random variables in many inferential problems, the logit-normal distribution is poorly studied. Most frustratingly, no default method exists for finding logit-normal moments, which are often assumed analytically unknown. In this paper, we introduce a method for estimating logit-normal moments of any positive integer order, based on approximating the logistic function. We will show our method is highly accurate up to the $8^\text{th}$ moment, avoids the numerical instability observed with Mordell integral based approximations of the first moment, and is faster than numerical integration in R. Focusing on two inferential applications, we will show our approximation methods are sufficiently accurate to enable faster implementation of Expectation Propagation for logistic regression, but is not general enough to directly evaluate the logistic normal integral that appears in some logistic mixed models.

</details>


### [189] [Bayesian Mixture Models for Histograms: with Applications to Large Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24001)
*Richard L. Warr,Fernando A. Quintana,Alessandra Guglielmi,Mario Beraha*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新颖的贝叶斯方法，通过对分箱数据拟合混合模型来推断潜在总体分布。方法支持有限和可数无限混合成分，利用可逆跳转MCMC进行推断，并扩展到使用狄利克雷过程同时建模和聚类多个直方图，实现跨群体信息共享与同质性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在涉及隐私约束或数据摘要的现实场景中，数据通常仅以聚合形式（如直方图或频率表）可用。传统的推断方法难以直接从这类分箱数据中恢复潜在的总体分布，因此需要一种能够处理聚合数据、灵活推断分布结构的方法。

Method: 该方法以正态分布混合模型为基础（可扩展至其他分布族），对混合成分数量设置先验分布以容纳有限和可数无限混合；采用可逆跳转MCMC（RJMCMC）进行后验推断；进一步利用狄里克雷过程将方法扩展至多直方图联合建模与聚类，通过后验概率评估群体间同质性。

Result: 该方法在大规模数据上表现出色，验证了非参数贝叶斯建模在聚合数据推断中的潜力。多直方图扩展能够有效实现信息共享，并提供有原则的群体同质性后验概率评估。文中还讨论了支撑该方法性能的部分理论结果。

Conclusion: 非参数贝叶斯框架在处理分箱数据的分布推断问题上具有显著优势，能够灵活适应未知数量的混合成分，并通过多群体联合建模进一步增强推断能力。该方法在实际应用中具有广泛前景。

Abstract: In many real-world scenarios, especially those involving privacy constraints or data summarization, data are available only in aggregated forms, such as histograms or frequency tables. This work introduces a novel Bayesian method for inferring the underlying population distribution by fitting a mixture model to binned data. While we focus on mixtures of normal distributions, the framework is flexible and can be extended to other distributional families. We place a prior distribution on the number of mixture components, accommodating both finite and countably infinite mixtures, and perform inference using reversible jump MCMC. The proposed approach demonstrates strong performance on large-scale data, showcasing the potential of nonparametric Bayesian modeling in practical applications. Furthermore, we extend the method to model multiple histograms simultaneously and cluster them using the Dirichlet process. This enables information sharing across populations and provides a principled posterior probability to assess homogeneity between groups. Some theoretical results supporting the performance of our proposed methodology are also discussed.

</details>


### [190] [Pseudo-value Based Mean Cumulative Count Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24024)
*Zachary R. McCaw,Alex Ocampo,Enrico Giudice,FengQi Song,Jessica Gronsbell*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 该文提出基于伪值回归的方法，用于在固定截断时间点估计协变量对平均累积函数（MCF）及其下面积（AUMCF）的效应，使用影响函数伪值作为回归结局，可借助广义估计方程或普通最小二乘进行估计，并通过模拟研究和ORATORIO临床试验应用验证了方法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MCF和AUMCF的推断方法主要集中于非参数处理比较、协变量校正增广和线性化检验统计量，缺乏灵活建模协变量效应的回归框架，尤其是对截断时间点处累积复发事件负担的协变量效应估计。

Method: 提出基于影响函数伪值的回归方法：将MCF和AUMCF在固定截断时间点的伪值作为回归结局，采用广义估计方程（GEE）进行参数估计；在恒等连接函数下可简化为普通最小二乘（OLS）估计，实现协变量效应的灵活建模。

Result: 模拟研究评估了估计准确性、置信区间覆盖率、I类错误控制和检验效能，结果显示该方法在多种复发事件场景下表现良好。通过ORATORIO临床试验（Ocrelizumab治疗原发进展型多发性硬化）的应用，展示了协变量校正程序的实用价值。

Conclusion: 伪值回归为建模协变量对累积复发事件负担的效应提供了一个简洁且可解释的框架，填补了MCF/AUMCF分析中缺乏灵活回归工具的方法学空白。

Abstract: The mean cumulative function (MCF) summarizes how events accumulate over time for a recurrent or multi-component endpoint. The MCF, and its integral over a given time horizon, the area under the MCF (AUMCF), provide interpretable summaries of recurrent-event burden in the presence of right-censoring and terminal events. Existing approaches for these estimands have focused primarily on nonparametric treatment comparisons, covariate-adjusted augmentation, and linearized test statistics. Herein, we propose a pseudo-value-based regression approach for estimating covariate effects on the MCF and AUMCF at a fixed truncation time. The proposed method uses influence-function-based pseudo-values as regression outcomes, allowing estimation with standard generalized estimating equation machinery and, under an identity link, ordinary least squares. Through simulation studies, we evaluate estimation accuracy, confidence interval coverage, type I error control, and power across a range of recurrent-event settings. We demonstrate the utility of the proposed covariate adjustment procedure through an application to the ORATORIO clinical trial, evaluating the safety and efficacy of ocrelizumab for the treatment of primary progressive multiple sclerosis. Overall, pseudo-value-based regression provides a simple and interpretable framework for modeling covariate effects on cumulative recurrent-event burden over time.

</details>


### [191] [A Non-Stationary Spatio-Temporal Covariance Model with Dynamic Advection Effects for Rainfall Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24076)
*Pedro Nasevicius Ramos,Guilherme Ludwig*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种基于时空协方差模型混合的非平稳建模方法，引入平流效应来模拟风向和云层移动，允许风向在估计的时间区间内发生变化，并通过MCMC贝叶斯估计对巴西东南部一次强降雨事件进行实证分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时空模型通常采用刚性（固定）的平流效应，无法捕捉实际气象数据中风向随时间变化的现象，因此需要一种能够允许平流方向动态变化的灵活非平稳时空模型。

Method: 构建一个混合时空协方差模型，其中各组分协方差沿特定方向向量具有更大的协方差值以模拟平流效应；通过混合多个此类模型实现风向在不同（估计的）时间区间的变化；采用马尔可夫链蒙特卡罗（MCMC）方法进行贝叶斯参数估计。

Result: 该混合模型能够有效捕捉数据中平流方向的变化，在对巴西东南部强降雨事件的分析中展示了方法的实用性和灵活性。

Conclusion: 基于混合时空协方差模型的非平稳建模框架，能够比传统刚性平流模型更真实地反映风向动态变化，在气象和环境时空数据分析中具有良好应用前景。

Abstract: We propose a non-stationary model constructed using a mixture of spatio-temporal covariance models with advection effects; namely, models that have larger covariance values along an orientation vector in the spatio-temporal index set, that simulate wind direction and cloud movement. We show that a mixture of such models can allow for wind direction change in data during (estimated) time intervals, unlike traditional models that use rigid advection effects. We construct a MCMC procedure for Bayesian estimation, and illustrate the method with the analysis of a severe rainfall event from the southeastern region of Brazil.

</details>


### [192] [Confounding analysis of s-level designs with multi-block variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24116)
*Wenbo Hu,Zhiming Li*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出区块化别名成分数模式（B²-ACNP），用于分析具有多区块变量的s水平设计的混杂性质，通过区块字长分布矩阵计算，统一现有分类准则，并提供算法与可视化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际实验中，区块变量常源于多种异质性来源，现有方法难以有效处理多区块变量下s水平设计的混杂问题，需要一个统一的框架来分析和刻画混杂结构。

Method: 提出区块化别名成分数模式（B²-ACNP），通过区块字长分布矩阵（blocked wordlength distribution matrix）计算其数值；将已有准则的分类模式表达为B²-ACNP中特定元素的函数，建立统一框架；并提供混杂算法与可视化方法。

Result: B²-ACNP能够有效刻画多区块变量下s水平设计的混杂性质；现有准则可在该统一框架下建立关联；案例分析验证了B²-ACNP的重要作用；附录提供了Python实现代码。

Conclusion: B²-ACNP为多区块变量s水平设计的混杂分析提供了一个统一、可计算且可视化的有效工具，具有理论和实践价值。

Abstract: In practical experiments, block variables often arise from multiple sources of heterogeneity. To address the confounding problem, this paper proposes a blocked aliased component-number pattern (B$^2$-ACNP) to analyze the confounding properties of s-level designs with multi-block variables. We calculate the values of (B$^2$-ACNP) via a blocked wordlength distribution matrix. The classification patterns of existing criteria can be expressed as functions of specific elements within the B$^2$-ACNP, thereby stablishing connections within a unified framework. Further, we provide confounding algorithms and visualization methods of the B$^2$-ACNP. Finally, case analysis clarifies the significant role of the B$^2$-ACNP. The Python code is available in the Appendix.

</details>


### [193] [When Surveys Become Conversations: Adaptive Matrix Validation for AI-Assisted Interviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24244)
*Tyler H. McCormick*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出自适应矩阵验证（AMV）框架：受访者先接受AI辅助访谈并由AI映射为结构化数据，再回答少量随机验证问题；利用这些验证数据对AI映射值进行统计校准与纠偏，从而估计项目均值、子群差异和回归系数，并给出所需验证问题数与样本量的规划公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助访谈将自然语言映射为结构化变量时存在测量误差，这种映射是含噪、版本化、自适应且可能在不同子群间表现不一致的，直接使用映射数据会导致估计偏差。需要一种统计设计来校准和纠正这些误差。

Method: AMV设计分两步：(1) 利用其他受访者的验证答案对AI映射值进行第一轮校准；(2) 利用目标受访者自身的验证答案纠正剩余误差。在此基础上推导了项目均值、子群估计以及回归系数（结果变量、预测变量或两者均来自访谈映射时的不同情形）的估计量，并给出验证问题数和样本量的规划公式。

Result: 通过设计校准模拟、美国时间使用调查仿真和CHAMPS口头尸检叙述研究三个实验表明：在稀疏验证条件下，AMV能够有效提升估计精度，但也揭示了稀疏验证无法改善精度的边界情形。

Conclusion: AMV为AI辅助访谈提供了一种统计上严谨的测量误差校正框架，实现了受访者负担减轻与数据质量保障之间的平衡，其适用性取决于验证数据的稀疏程度和误差结构。

Abstract: AI-assisted interviews promise to reduce respondent burden in surveys by allowing respondents to describe experiences naturally while an AI system noisily maps those accounts into structured survey variables. That mapping is a measurement process that is fallible, versioned, adaptive, and potentially behaves differently across subgroups. This paper proposes Adaptive Matrix Validation (AMV), a design in which each respondent completes an AI-assisted interview, which is then mapped into tabular data by the AI. Respondents are also asked a small, randomized set of structured questions, which are used for statistical adjustment. The estimator first calibrates the mapped values using validation answers from other respondents, then corrects the remaining error with the validation answers observed for the target respondent. The paper develops estimators for item means, subgroup estimates, and regression coefficients when outcomes, predictors, or both are mapped from interviews. It also gives planning formulas the number of validation questions required and the sample size. A design-calibration simulation, an American Time Use Survey emulation, and a CHAMPS verbal-autopsy narrative study show when sparse validation can improve precision and when it cannot

</details>


### [194] [Uncertainty intervals for multilevel models with missing not at random data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24228)
*Minna Genbäck*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对线性多水平模型中非随机缺失（MNAR）数据的敏感性分析方法，通过设定敏感参数范围对感兴趣参数进行部分识别，得到估计和推断的界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多水平模型在纵向数据分析中广泛应用，但现有方法多假设随机缺失（MAR）。当缺失机制为非随机缺失（MNAR）时，MAR假设下的估计可能产生严重偏倚，因此需要一种能在较MAR更弱的假设下进行推断的敏感性分析方法。

Method: 对结局和脱落风险均采用多水平模型建模，推导出因MNAR数据导致的偏倚调整量。该偏倚可从观测数据中基于指定的敏感参数值进行估计。通过假设这些参数在一个合理的范围内，实现对感兴趣参数的部分识别，得到估计和推断的界限。

Result: 通过模拟研究验证了所提方法的性能，并以孤独感、体力活动与记忆轨迹之间的关联分析为例进行了实证说明，分析中调整了人口统计学、社会经济和健康协变量。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在比MAR更弱的假设条件下，通过设定合理的敏感参数范围，为多水平模型中MNAR数据提供有界限的估计和推断，增强了研究结论的稳健性。

Abstract: We propose a sensitivity analysis method for missing not at random (MNAR) data in the context of linear multilevel (mixed-effects) models. The outcome and dropout risk are both modelled using multilevel models and a bias adjustment due to MNAR data is derived. This bias can be estimated from observed data conditional on specified values of sensitivity parameter(s). Under the assumption that these parameters lie within a plausible range, the method partially identify the parameters of interest, yielding bounds for estimation and inference under assumptions weaker than missing at random. The proposed analysis is investigated in a simulation study and illustrated with an analysis of the association between loneliness and physical activity with memory trajectories, adjusting for demographic, socioeconomic, and health covariates.

</details>


### [195] [Prediction of spatio-temporal data on meshed surfaces using advection-diffusion SPDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24300)
*Mike Pereira,Lucia Clarotto,Nicolas Desassis*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于SPDE方法的网格表面上时空数据的统计模型，专注于二维光滑紧致可定向闭黎曼流形上的平流-扩散SPDE，并通过Galerkin方法进行离散化，实现了对高斯随机场的可扩展模拟与预测，并应用于球面模拟数据和全球气溶胶光学厚度实测数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种能够在网格化曲面（如球面）上高效处理具有平流和扩散行为的时空数据的统计模型，解决传统方法在复杂几何结构上可扩展性不足的问题。

Method: 基于随机偏微分方程（SPDE）方法，在二维光滑紧致可定向闭黎曼流形上定义一类平流-扩散SPDE；采用Galerkin方法进行离散化，实现对解为高斯随机场的可扩展模拟与预测算法，并引入最新的推断方法。

Result: 该方法在具有平流和扩散特征的球面时空模拟数据集上得到验证，并成功应用于全球大气气溶胶光学厚度的实际案例研究。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于SPDE的建模方法能够在曲面几何结构上高效进行时空高斯随机场的模拟、预测与推断，具有良好的可扩展性和实际应用价值。

Abstract: The aim of this work is to propose a statistical model for spatio-temporal data on meshed surfaces based on the Stochastic Partial Differential Equation (SPDE) modeling approach. Specifically, we focus on a class of advection-diffusion SPDEs defined on smooth compact orientable closed Riemannian manifolds of dimension 2, and their discretization via a Galerkin approach. We demonstrate how this method enables the development of scalable algorithms for the simulation and prediction of Gaussian random fields that are solutions to the discretized SPDE. Additionally, we present recent developments in the inference of such models. The method is applied to a simulated spatio-temporal dataset exhibiting advective and diffusive behavior on the sphere, as well as to a real case study on aerosol optical depth in the atmosphere across the globe's surface.

</details>


### [196] [Bayesian Variable Selection in Generalized Linear Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24357)
*Lucia Filippozzi,Iñigo Urteaga,Claudio Agostinelli*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于广义线性模型（GLM）的完全贝叶斯层次共轭框架，通过二元包含指示变量进行协变量选择，兼具理论上的后验一致性保证和高效的Gibbs采样算法实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯变量选择方法（如spike-and-slab先验、连续收缩先验）存在三大局限：(1) 非完全共轭形式导致计算复杂；(2) 多局限于线性模型，难以推广到GLM；(3) 缺乏变量选择和模型参数的后验一致性理论保证。因此需要一种既能覆盖指数族分布、又具备理论保证和计算效率的统一框架。

Method: 作者提出了一种完全贝叶斯层次共轭框架：通过建模二元包含指示变量（binary inclusion indicator），直接在线性预测中编码协变量的包含与否。该框架适用于指数族中的任意分布，变量选择与参数估计同步进行，并将两类不确定性纳入后验推断。文章还推导了高效的Gibbs采样算法，并开发了对应的R包。

Result: 理论层面，文章建立了所提方法在GLM下的后验一致性保证——涵盖包含指示变量和活跃回归系数两者。实验层面，在合成数据和真实世界数据集上验证了该方法在预测和推断方面均具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 该工作为GLM中的协变量选择提供了一个兼具完全共轭性、广泛分布适用性、后验一致性理论保证和计算可行性的统一贝叶斯框架，填补了现有方法的空白。

Abstract: Covariate selection in Generalized Linear Models (GLMs) is a fundamental problem in statistics, as including irrelevant predictors might lead to overfitting and poor interpretability, while omitting relevant ones might result in biased estimates. Most Bayesian approaches to variable selection -- including spike-and-slab priors and continuous shrinkage priors -- have key limitations, e.g., (i) are based on non fully conjugate formulations, (ii) are restricted to a linear model, or (iii) lack posterior consistency guarantees for the variable selection procedure and model parameters. In this work, we propose a fully Bayesian hierarchical and conjugate framework for covariate selection in GLMs, applicable to any distribution in the exponential family, based on modeling a binary inclusion indicator that directly encodes covariate inclusion in the linear predictor. In our approach, variable selection and parameter estimation are performed simultaneously, incorporating both sources of uncertainty in posterior inference. Consequently, our methodology provides a valid post-model Bayesian selection procedure. We present theoretical guarantees of the proposed fully conjugate Bayesian variable selection for GLMs, establishing posterior consistency of both the inclusion indicators and the active regression coefficients. We derive an efficient Gibbs Sampling algorithm with a corresponding R package implementation. We validate the proposed method on synthetic and real-world datasets, demonstrating competitive predictive and inferential performance.

</details>


### [197] [ICSpyLab: A Python package for invariant coordinate selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24521)
*Colombe Becquart*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: ICSpyLab 是首个实现不变坐标选择（ICS）的 Python 包，提供丰富的散点矩阵、不变分量计算算法和分量选择标准，旨在将 ICS 无缝集成到现代机器学习工作流中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 ICS 在聚类和异常检测中具有理论和实践价值，但目前缺乏专用的 Python 实现，限制了其在现代机器学习工作流中的应用。

Method: 开发 ICSpyLab 包，基于两种散点矩阵的联合对角化实现 ICS，集成多种散点矩阵、多个不变分量计算算法以及多种分量选择标准，遵循标准估计器接口设计。

Result: ICSpyLab 作为首个 ICS 的 Python 实现，提供了完整的文档、可复现示例，并在 MIT 许可证下开源发布。

Conclusion: ICSpyLab 填补了 ICS 在 Python 生态中的空白，兼具易用性和可扩展性，适合实践者和研究者使用，有望推动 ICS 在更广泛场景中的应用。

Abstract: Invariant coordinate selection (ICS) is a dimensionality reduction technique based on the joint diagonalization of two scatter matrices. While principal component analysis relies solely on variance, ICS seeks directions of maximal or minimal generalized kurtosis, making it a powerful alternative for clustering and anomaly detection. Despite its theoretical and practical relevance, no dedicated Python implementation of ICS is currently available, limiting its integration into modern machine-learning workflows. We introduce ICSpyLab, the first Python package implementing ICS. It provides a broad collection of scatter matrices, multiple algorithms for computing invariant components, and several component-selection criteria. Designed for both practitioners and researchers, ICSpyLab follows a standard estimator interface, facilitating its use within machine-learning pipelines while remaining flexible for methodological extensions. The documentation includes detailed explanations and reproducible examples. ICSpyLab is released under the MIT license and is openly available at: https://github.com/cbecquart/ICSpyLab.

</details>


### [198] [Partial Wavelet Canonical Coherence for Nonstationary Signals with High Dimensional Confounders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24554)
*Haibo Wu,Marina I. Knight,Hernando Ombao*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了偏小波典型相干性方法，用于在控制高维混杂因素后测量两个多元非平稳时间序列之间的直接典型关联。该方法通过小波分析实现了频率域上的偏典型相关，能够提供尺度特定、时变的关联度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法无法在频率域中对多元非平稳时间序列进行偏典型相关分析，即无法在控制高维混杂因素后测量两个多变量序列之间的直接关联。这一缺陷限制了在经济学、金融学等领域中识别变量间真实直接关系的能力。

Method: 作者在多元局部平稳小波框架下构建了偏小波典型相干性的目标量，通过局部小波谱矩阵进行估计，并引入主成分降维技术以实现高维混杂因素下的稳定调节。该方法能够产生尺度特定且时变的关联测度。

Result: 模拟实验表明，该方法能有效消除由混杂引起的虚假边缘关联，并准确恢复直接关联，在高维场景下同样表现良好。对美国交易所交易基金（ETF）的实证分析揭示了在控制外部市场效应后，ETF之间存在显著的时变且尺度依赖的直接典型关联。

Conclusion: 偏小波典型相干性是首个在频率域中实现偏典型相关分析的方法，能够有效处理非平稳时间序列，在控制高维混杂因素后提供稳健的时变、多尺度直接关联度量，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: We develop Partial Wavelet Canonical Coherence for measuring the direct canonical association between two multivariate nonstationary time series after adjustment for possibly high-dimensional confounders. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first method that establishes a frequency-domain formulation of the partial canonical correlation analysis for time series. Through a wavelet approach, the proposed method yields a scale-specific, time-varying measure of association capable to work with potential data nonstationarities. We formulate the target quantity under the multivariate locally stationary wavelet framework, develop principled estimation through local wavelet spectral matrices, and incorporate principal-component reduction for stable adjustment in high-dimensions. Simulations show that the method removes spurious marginal association induced by confounding and accurately recovers direct association, including in higher-dimensional settings. Analysis of U.S. exchange-traded funds reveals substantial time-varying and scale-dependent direct canonical association after adjustment for external market effects.

</details>


### [199] [Bayesian Estimation of the Eigenstructure in High-Dimensional Approximate Factor Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24652)
*Seongmin Kim,Jaeyong Lee*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对高维近似因子模型中样本协方差矩阵特征值与特征向量严重失真、导致主成分估计不稳定的问题，本文提出一种贝叶斯方法估计近似因子结构，并证明其后验收敛速率与高维尖峰协方差模型的基准结果同阶，模拟和实际数据均验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维经济数据中，当变量数远大于样本量时，样本协方差矩阵的特征值和特征向量会发生严重扭曲，使得基于主成分分析的因子结构估计变得不稳定，需要一种在高维设定下依然可靠且具备不确定性量化能力的因子结构估计方法。

Method: 提出一个贝叶斯近似因子模型，对因子载荷和潜在因子施加合理的先验分布，通过后验推断恢复因子结构，并从理论上推导了后验收敛速率，将其与高维尖峰协方差模型（spiked covariance model）的已知最优收敛速率进行对比。

Result: 理论证明后验收敛速率与高维尖峰协方差模型的基准结果处于同一阶数。模拟研究表明，该方法在恢复近似因子结构方面比现有方法更准确。宏观金融数据实证分析显示，该方法能提供可解释的潜在因子结构估计，并在预测任务中具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 所提出的贝叶斯方法有效解决了高维近似因子模型中主成分估计不稳定问题，兼具理论保证和实用价值，为高维宏观经济与金融数据的因子建模提供了更可靠的工具。

Abstract: High-dimensional economic datasets often display strong co-movement driven by a small number of latent factors, which are typically modeled using approximate factor models. When the number of variables is large relative to the sample size, the eigenvalues and eigenvectors of the sample covariance matrix are severely distorted, which in turn makes principal component based estimators of the factor structure unstable. To address the high-dimensional problem, we propose a Bayesian model for approximate factor structures. We show that the posterior convergence rate is of the same order as benchmark results for high-dimensional spiked covariance models. Simulation studies show that the proposed method more accurately recovers the factor structure in approximate factor models than existing methods. Real data analyses on macro--financial datasets illustrate that the proposed method provides interpretable estimates of latent factor structure and performs competitively in forecasting exercises.

</details>


### [200] [Landmarking with Latent Class Mixed Models for Dynamic Prediction of Time-to-event Data with Heterogeneous Biomarker Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24678)
*Víctor Velasco-Pardo,Nathan Constantine-Cooke,Charlie W. Lees,Catalina A. Vallejos*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合潜在类别混合模型（LCMM）的新型landmarking方法，用于在存在潜在异质性的纵向数据中进行动态风险预测。该方法在R包landmaRk中实现，仿真研究表明其在预测性能上优于传统landmarking策略，同时保持计算高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录（EHR）时代，大规模异质性患者群体的纵向生物标志物数据可用于动态风险预测。然而，现有动态预测方法（如传统landmarking）通常假设个体纵向轨迹同质，无法处理由未观测混杂因素导致的潜在亚组结构。而能够处理此类异质性的联合潜在类别模型（JLCM）计算强度过大，不适用于EHR规模的数据。因此，需要一种既能捕捉潜在异质性又计算高效的动态风险预测方法。

Method: 提出一种将潜在类别混合模型（LCMM）与landmarking框架相结合的新方法：首先利用LCMM对纵向生物标志物轨迹进行建模，捕捉潜在的亚组异质性结构；然后将提取的潜在类别信息整合到landmarking动态预测框架中，实现随时间更新的风险预测。该方法已实现为模块化R包landmaRk（已发布至CRAN），支持用户灵活定制landmarking分析的各组件。

Result: 仿真研究表明，当数据中存在潜在异质性时，所提方法在预测性能上显著优于忽略异质性的传统landmarking策略；同时，该方法保持了高计算效率，适用于大规模数据集。此外，还通过真实数据提供了概念验证性示例。

Conclusion: 所提出的LCMM-landmarking集成方法有效解决了大规模纵向数据动态风险预测中潜在异质性的建模问题，在预测准确性与计算可行性之间取得了良好平衡。landmaRk R包的发布也为该方法的推广应用提供了便利。

Abstract: The increasing ability to securely access electronic health records (EHR) has created unprecedented opportunities to monitor the health trajectories of large heterogeneous patient populations throughout their lifetime. Repeated measurements of time-varying covariates (such as biomarkers measured via routine blood tests) can inform dynamic risk prediction of time-to-event outcomes, updating risk estimates as new information becomes available. Existing dynamic risk prediction approaches often assume homogeneous longitudinal trajectories across individuals. This assumption is not met when there is heterogeneity driven by latent subgroup structure (e.g. due to unobserved confounders), as is often the case with real-world biomedical data. At present, accounting for such heterogeneity is only available in joint latent class models for longitudinal and time-to-event data, but they are computationally intensive, often prohibitively so for large-scale data, such as those present in EHR settings. To address these challenges, we propose a novel landmarking approach that integrates latent class mixed models (LCMMs) to capture latent heterogeneity in longitudinal trajectories. Our method is implemented in a modular R package, landmaRk, which is available on CRAN and allows users to flexibly specify the components of a landmarking analysis, beyond our proposed approach. Through simulation studies, we demonstrate improvements in prediction performance in the presence of latent heterogeneity compared to traditional landmarking strategies, while remaining computationally efficient for large datasets. We also provide a proof-of-concept illustration using real data.

</details>


### [201] [Hierarchical Bayesian Estimation of Covariance Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24751)
*Daniel Xiang,Malgorzata Bogdan,Jonas Wallin,Daniel Yekutieli*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于分层贝叶斯框架的协方差矩阵估计方法。核心洞察是：相对于完全一般线性群GL(p)的等变性极为受限，只能得到样本协方差的标量倍估计；而常用的收缩估计量仅满足正交群O(p)等变性。作者在O(p)等变估计类中，建立了Haar测度贝叶斯规则作为最小风险估计，并推导了三种损失函数下的oracle贝叶斯规则。为逼近这些oracle规则，引入有限Pólya树先验的分层贝叶斯模型，通过Gibbs采样得到后验估计，模拟表明该方法显著优于经典竞争者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协方差矩阵估计中，要么满足严格但过于受限的GL(p)等变性导致估计质量差，要么使用缺乏理论基准的收缩估计量。作者旨在利用O(p)等变性这一更弱的对称性结构，建立理论最优的oracle基准，并通过分层贝叶斯方法实现可操作的近似。

Method: 首先在O(p)等变估计类中，利用Haar测度推导出oracle特征值模型下的贝叶斯规则，作为最小风险估计。然后在平方Frobenius损失、Stein损失和平方Stein损失下，分别推导协方差矩阵和精度矩阵的oracle贝叶斯规则。为处理真实特征值未知的问题，构建分层贝叶斯模型：对特征值分布赋予有限Pólya树先验，通过Gibbs采样生成后验抽取，同时获得特征值的收缩估计和oracle贝叶斯规则的近似。

Result: 有限Pólya树先验能够恢复特征值分布的一般形式；所提估计量在模拟中接近oracle性能，在协方差和精度矩阵估计上均显著优于包括Haff经验贝叶斯估计和Ledoit-Wolf估计在内的经典收缩估计量。

Conclusion: 利用O(p)等变性可以建立协方差/精度矩阵估计的理论最优基准，而分层贝叶斯框架结合有限Pólya树先验能够有效逼近这些基准，提供了一种理论上合理且实践上优越的估计方法。

Abstract: We develop a hierarchical Bayesian framework for covariance matrix estimation built on a key observation: while equivariance under the full general linear group GL(p) is well known, it is an extremely restrictive property -- estimators equivariant to GL(p) are limited to scalar multiples of the sample covariance matrix and carry considerably larger risks than shrinkage estimators. By contrast, commonly used shrinkage estimators, including the Haff empirical Bayes estimator, and the Ledoit--Wolf estimators, are all equivariant under the smaller orthogonal group O(p). Exploiting this structure, we establish that the Haar measure Bayes rule in an oracle eigenvalue model is the minimum risk estimator within the class of O(p)-equivariant estimators, and derive oracle Bayes rules for the covariance and precision matrices under the squared Frobenius, Stein, and squared Stein loss functions. These oracle rules serve as theoretical benchmarks that dominate all commonly used estimators. To approximate them when the true eigenvalues are unknown, we introduce a hierarchical Bayes model that places a finite P'olya tree prior on the eigenvalue distribution and uses Gibbs sampling to generate posterior draws, yielding both shrinkage estimates for the eigenvalues and approximations to the oracle Bayes rules. Simulations suggest that the finite P'olya tree prior is able to recover the general form of the distribution of the eigenvalues, and confirm that the resulting estimators closely approach oracle performance, substantially outperforming classical competitors for both covariance and precision matrix estimation.

</details>


### [202] [Sequential Probability Ratio Test using Z-Statistics (SPRT-z): A Practical Approach for Online Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24871)
*Derek L. Ho,Emma G. Thomas*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文复兴了Wald的序贯概率比检验(SPRT)，提出三项创新：SPRT-z利用大样本正态近似消除计算瓶颈；无标度地平线校准(SFHC)在标准化Z尺度上设定最大样本量以保持名义功效；布朗运动无偏估计量及置信区间纠正提前停止导致的偏差。仿真显示该方法能控制I/II类错误，减少样本量，并改善估计偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代在线实验平台持续产生大规模数据，但从业者常在重复窥探下使用固定地平线检验(FHT)，导致I类错误膨胀和决策质量下降。现有始终有效的序贯方法虽能在窥探下控制I类错误并支持早期有效性停止，但并不原生支持早期无效性停止、与业务相关的最小可检测效应挂钩的启动标准，或II类错误控制。

Method: 作者提出三项创新：(1) SPRT-z：对Hajnal序贯t检验的改进，利用大样本正态近似消除计算瓶颈，并为后续布朗运动方法奠定基础；(2) SFHC：在标准化Z尺度上进行蒙特卡洛二分法，设定离散监控下保持名义功效的最大样本量；(3) 布朗中位数无偏估计量及置信区间：通过六区域阶段排序对样本空间进行划分，纠正所有停止区域中由提前停止引起的向上偏差。

Result: 仿真研究表明，该工作流在大多数研究场景中能适当控制I类和II类错误，与FHT相比减少了所需样本量，并改善了提前停止带来的估计偏差，置信区间覆盖接近名义水平。

Conclusion: SPRT工作流为在线实验提供了一个兼具早期有效性/无效性停止、业务相关MDE设定、I/II类错误控制以及偏差校正估计的完整方案，是固定地平线检验的有效替代。

Abstract: Modern online experimentation platforms produce data at scale and continuously. However, practitioners routinely apply Fixed Horizon Testing (FHT) under repeated peeking, inflating Type I error and reducing decision quality. Popular always valid sequential methods control Type I error under peeking and enable early stopping for efficacy, but do not natively support early futility stopping, launch criteria tied to a business-relevant minimum detectable effect, or Type II error control. As an alternative that satisfies these useful properties, we revive Wald's Sequential Probability Ratio Test (SPRT) for online experimentation with three novel contributions: (1) SPRT-z, an adaptation of Hajnal's sequential $t$-test, leverages large sample normal approximation to eliminate computational bottlenecks inherent to the scale of modern A/B tests and enables the Brownian motion-based methods used in (2) and (3); (2) Scale-Free Horizon Calibration (SFHC) is a Monte Carlo bisection procedure on the standardised $Z$-scale that sets a maximum sample size preserving nominal power under discrete monitoring with futility stopping; (3) A Brownian Median Unbiased Estimator and accompanying confidence intervals correct the upward bias induced by early stopping across all stopping regions via a six-region stagewise ordering of the sample space. A simulation study shows this workflow appropriately controls Type I and II error, reduces sample size relative to FHT, and ameliorates estimation bias from early stopping with close-to-nominal confidence interval coverage in most scenarios studied.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [203] [Systematic Exploration of 4-Expert Heterogeneous Mixture-of-Experts via Automated Pipeline Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23739)
*Yashkumar R Lukhi,Harsh Rameshbhai Moradiya,Radu Timofte,Dmitry Ignatov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个自动化大规模搜索流水线，用于在LEMUR神经网络数据集生态系统中探索异构4专家混合（MoE4）架构。在28天里于单张RTX 4090上生成了4,463个候选模型（1,021个成功评估），发现由于字母枚举导致搜索空间被AirNet单一家族锚定（覆盖率仅4.8%），并识别出ShuffleNet与MobileNetV3组合产生最高精度，FractalNet和MNASNet为低收益家族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将手工设计的异构MoE参考模型替换为自动化的确定性代码组装生成器，以系统性地探索LEMUR数据库中基础架构家族组合构成的MoE4集成空间，消除人工设计瓶颈。

Method: 构建确定性代码组装生成器，利用itertools.combinations从LEMUR数据库中系统组合基础架构家族形成MoE4集成；每个集成由卷积门控网络控制，并采用温度缩放、mixup增强和余弦退火学习率调度；在NVIDIA RTX 4090上进行28天搜索，分197批次生成4,463个候选模型。

Result: （1）搜索空间覆盖率仅4.8%（理论23,751种组合），且因字母枚举全部锚定于AirNet家族；（2）在AirNet锚定范围内，ShuffleNet与MobileNetV3组合一致产生最高精度集成（平均准确率达0.632）；（3）FractalNet与MNASNet被识别为低收益家族，建议未来排除。

Conclusion: 字母枚举引入的覆盖偏差严重限制了搜索空间探索的有效性；作者提出采用分层随机采样修复该问题，并将整个流水线、分析产物及修正后的生成器以开源形式发布于NNGPT项目。

Abstract: We present an automated large-scale search pipeline for heterogeneous 4-Expert Mixture-of-Experts (MoE4) architectures within the LEMUR neural network dataset ecosystem. Building on a hand-crafted heterogeneous MoE reference model, we replace manual design with a deterministic code-assembly generator that systematically combines base architecture families drawn from the LEMUR database into MoE4 ensembles, each governed by a convolutional gating network with temperature scaling, mixup augmentation, and cosine-annealed learning rate scheduling. Over a 28-day campaign on an NVIDIA RTX 4090, the pipeline generated 4,463 candidate models across 197 batches, of which 1,021 were evaluated successfully. A critical finding emerged from the campaign: due to alphabetical enumeration via itertools.combinations, the entire explored search space (4.8% of the theoretical 23,751 possible 4-family combinations) is anchored to a single family, AirNet. We characterise this coverage bias precisely, identify the root cause in the generator, and propose a stratified random sampling fix. Within the AirNet anchored scope, ShuffleNet and MobileNetV3 consistently co-produce the highest-accuracy ensembles (mean accuracy up to 0.632), while FractalNet and MNASNet are identified as low-yield families warranting exclusion in future campaigns. The pipeline, analysis artefacts, and corrected generator are released as part of the open-source NNGPT project at https://github.com/ABrain-One/nn-gpt

</details>


### [204] [Weight-Space Geometry of Offline Reasoning Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23740)
*Aleksandr Nikolich,Igor Kiselev,Vladimir Platonov,Karina Romanova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过分析六种离线强化学习损失函数（SFT、RFT、DFT、RIFT、Offline GRPO、DPO）在相同数据上训练后的权重更新，发现它们在几何上存在显著差异：SFT/RFT/RIFT 几乎共线，DFT 略有偏离，Offline GRPO 包含大量正交分量但仍处于同一损失盆地，而 DPO 几乎正交且具有模态连通性障碍，同时 DPO 实现了最高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前离线强化学习损失函数（如 RFT、DPO、GRPO 等）在知识蒸馏任务中通常仅以下游准确率进行比较，缺乏对它们是否在机制上趋同或产生不同权重更新的理解。作者旨在通过几何分析揭示这些方法是否本质上收敛到相似的解。

Method: 在统一设置下（Qwen3-4B 基座模型、相同的数学推理 rollouts、仅训练注意力层的 LoRA），分别用六种方法训练模型，然后通过四种分析手段对比权重增量：余弦相似度、主角度子空间分析、线性模态连通性（LMC）以及中心核对齐（CKA）。

Result: 结果表明：(1) SFT、RFT、RIFT 的权重增量几乎共线（余弦相似度 ≥ 0.97，主角度中位数约 7°），GSM8K 准确率相当（87-88%）；(2) DFT 偏离方向大于任何奖励加权方法；(3) Offline GRPO 在 SFT 方向上叠加了大量正交分量（全局约 67%，后期层高达 86%），但仍处于 SFT 的损失盆地中；(4) DPO 处于近乎正交的子空间，存在模态连通性障碍，后期层 CKA 降至约 0.46，但准确率最高（GSM8K 93.5%，AIME26 30.0%）。DPO 使用的学习率比其他方法小 10 倍，需注意这一混杂因素。

Conclusion: 不同离线 RL 损失函数产生的权重更新在几何上远非同质：简单加权方法（SFT/RFT/RIFT）趋于共线，而 DPO 和 Offline GRPO 引入了显著的正交结构。DPO 的几何独特性与最高准确率相关，但学习率差异使得因果归因复杂化，等学习率比较留待未来工作。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement-learning losses (RFT, RIFT, DFT, Offline GRPO, DPO) are widely used to distill reasoning from large teachers into smaller students, and are typically compared on downstream accuracy alone. We ask whether they are mechanistically distinct or converge to a similar weight update. Training six methods (SFT, RFT, DFT, RIFT, Offline GRPO, DPO) on identical math rollouts from a single base model (Qwen3-4B) with attention-only LoRA, we analyze the resulting deltas via cosine similarity, principal-angle subspace analysis, linear mode connectivity, and CKA. We observe: (i) SFT, RFT, and RIFT have nearly colinear weight deltas (cosine >= 0.97, top-1 principal angle ~7 deg median over 144 modules) and comparable GSM8K accuracy (87-88%, n=1319; pairwise McNemar p >= 0.15); (ii) DFT diverges further in direction than any reward-weighted method despite using the same data; (iii) Offline GRPO adds a substantial component orthogonal to the SFT direction (~67% globally, up to ~86% in late layers) while staying in the SFT loss basin; (iv) DPO sits in a near-orthogonal subspace, shows a mode-connectivity barrier, and collapses late-layer CKA to ~0.46. DPO also reaches the highest accuracy in our protocol on both GSM8K (93.5%, McNemar p < 10^-9 vs. each other method) and AIME26 (30.0% vs. 3.3-10.0%); its training uses a 10x smaller learning rate than the others (the standard convention), so the update-norm and accuracy gaps reflect loss-function and optimizer choices jointly, and a learning-rate-matched DPO comparison is left for future work.

</details>


### [205] [The Degeneracy Distillery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23838)
*T. Lucas Makinen,Deaglan J. Bartlett,Niall Jeffrey,Benjamin D. Wandelt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出“简并蒸馏器”方法，通过估计和平坦化Fisher信息矩阵，从参数-数据对中自动且符号化地检测和解析简并参数组合。该方法将简并表征为物理模型的内蕴属性，无需实际观测数据，能在全局平坦化Fisher信息，显著降低下游神经后验估计所需模拟量（最多10倍减少），同时获得系统物理洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当模型参数或标签产生相似数据时形成“简并”，导致参数辨识和逆问题求解困难。现有方法（如基于后验的方法）仅能在单点局部平坦化，且需大量模拟。需要一种无需实际数据、能全局检测并符号化解析简并的自动化方法，以降低模拟成本并提供物理解释。

Method: 提出“简并蒸馏器”（degeneracy distillery），核心步骤：(1) 通过估计Fisher信息矩阵（FIM）检测简并——利用参数-数据（或参数-模拟）对，探索似然的信息几何；(2) 通过平坦化FIM来解析简并，自动发现符号化坐标变换，识别产生独立数据效应的参数组合。该方法将简并视为物理模型的内蕴属性，无需实际观测数据，且能在期望意义上全局平坦化Fisher信息。

Result: 在多个合成和真实问题上验证：方法成功发现符号化坐标变换，识别出对数据产生独立效应的参数组合；相比仅在单点平坦化的后验方法，实现了Fisher信息的全局平坦化；在下游神经后验估计中，达到匹配验证校准精度的同时，所需模拟量最多减少10倍。

Conclusion: 简并蒸馏器能有效自动检测和符号化解构物理模型中的参数简并，既降低后验估计模拟成本，又提供系统物理洞察，为模型选择和理解数据生成过程提供了有力工具。

Abstract: When two or more parameters or labels produce similar data, they are degenerate, or hard to distinguish. Degeneracies render both label prediction and inverse problems difficult, since both machine learning algorithms and probabilistic samplers rely on the distinguishability of data and its gradients with respect to parameters. However, identifying degeneracies in physical models or real-world datasets can be elucidating about the choice of model or the underlying process that produces the data. We present the degeneracy distillery, a method that (1) detects and (2) resolves degenerate parameter combinations (a) automatically and (b) symbolically, from parameter-data (or parameter-simulation) pairs alone, through estimation and flattening of the Fisher information matrix. By exploring the information geometry of the likelihood, we characterize degeneracies as an intrinsic property of the physical model, requiring no realised data observation. We demonstrate our approach on a range of synthetic and real-world problems, discovering symbolic coordinate transformations that identify the combinations of parameters of a model which yield independent effects on the data. The resulting coordinates flatten the Fisher information in expectation globally, in contrast to posterior-based methods that flatten only at a single point, and substantially reduce the simulation budget required for downstream neural posterior estimation. In test cases we require up to $10\times$ fewer simulations for posterior estimation at matched validation calibration whilst simultaneously gaining physical insight on the system.

</details>


### [206] [A Survey on Federated Causal Discovery and Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23741)
*Xianjie Guo,Yuwei Wang,Guodu Xiang,Xiaoli Tang,Kui Yu,Han Yu,Qiang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了联邦因果推理领域，涵盖联邦因果发现（FCD）与联邦因果推断（FCI）两大子领域。作者提出多维度分类法：FCD按方法论范式、联邦拓扑和结构范围三轴组织；FCI按目标估计量和估计策略分类。文中首次将FCD与FCI形式化为统一联邦因果推理管线的互补阶段，并总结了隐私、通信效率、理论保证等共同关注点及未来开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果推理（因果发现与因果推断）对数据驱动决策至关重要，但实际中数据常因隐私法规或通信限制而分散于多个机构，无法集中分析。联邦学习虽能实现协作分析，催生了联邦因果发现（FCD）与联邦因果推断（FCI）这一快速增长领域，但该领域的跨学科性及缺乏全面综述的现状，给研究者进入该领域造成障碍。

Method: 本文采用系统综述方法，基于多维度分类法进行组织。对于FCD，围绕三个核心设计决策——结构如何学习、数据如何划分、各方获取何种结构知识——确立三条分类轴：方法论范式（methodological paradigm）、联邦拓扑（federation topology）和结构范围（structural scope）；并进一步考察时序动态、数据异质性、缺失数据及非一致变量集等实践维度。对于FCI，按目标估计量（平均 vs. 个体化/条件处理效应）和估计策略（从经典加权方法到现代深度生成架构）进行分类。最终将FCD与FCI形式化为统一管线中的互补阶段。

Result: 构建了一个覆盖FCD与FCI的全面分类体系，明确了FCD为FCI提供结构知识以支持有效效应估计的互补关系，并归纳出两者在隐私保护、通信效率、理论保证和应用领域等方面的共同关切。

Conclusion: 联邦因果发现与联邦因果推断是统一联邦因果推理管线中紧密关联的两个阶段。该领域仍面临诸多开放挑战，包括隐私与效用的权衡、通信效率优化、异构数据处理及理论保证的完善等，有待未来研究深入探索。

Abstract: Causal reasoning, which encompasses the discovery of causal structures and the inference of causal effects, is fundamental to data-driven decision making. In practice, data for reliable causal analysis are often distributed across institutions and cannot be centralized due to privacy regulations or communication constraints. Federated learning (FL) addresses this by enabling collaborative analysis without raw data sharing, giving rise to the rapidly growing field of federated causal discovery (FCD) and inference (FCI). However, the interdisciplinary nature of this field and the absence of a comprehensive survey present barriers to entry for researchers.
